부가가치세 화면 소스 (개발자 전달용) - 2026-11-06

※ 화면별 단일 HTML 파일(HTML+CSS+JS) 소스입니다. 각 구역을 별도 .html 파일로 저장하면 단독 실행됩니다.
※ 화면 동작·입력 방식·연동 규칙은 '부가가치세화면모음_주석.html'(개발자 주석)과 '부가가치세_화면설계_엑셀.xlsx'를 함께 참고하세요.
※ 샘플 데이터(금액·거래처 등)는 예시이며 실제 연동 값이 아닙니다.
※ 마감 연동은 신고서(일반) -> 부속명세 postMessage {type:'vat-set-closed'} 방식의 목업 구현입니다.

const LBL={};FORM.forEach(r=>{if(r.n)LBL[r.n]=r.l});
function tcell(kind,key,label){
  if(kind==='off')return '<td class="offc"></td>';
  if(kind==='in')return '<td><input class="cell in" data-edit data-k="'+key+'" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" readonly aria-label="'+label+'"></td>';
  return '<td><input class="cell" data-k="'+key+'" readonly tabindex="-1" aria-label="'+label+'"></td>';
}
function fRow(r){
  const k=r.k!==undefined?r.k:r.n;
  const nm=r.n?'('+r.n+') ':'';
  let h='<tr'+(r.n?' data-n="'+r.n+'"':'')+' class="'+(r.total?'total ':'')+(r.grand?'grand':'')+'">';
  if(r.g1)h+='<th class="g1" rowspan="'+r.g1[1]+'" scope="rowgroup"><span>'+r.g1[0]+'</span></th>';
  if(r.g2)h+='<th class="g2" rowspan="'+r.g2[1]+'" scope="rowgroup"><span>'+r.g2[0]+'</span></th>';
  h+='<td class="lb" colspan="'+r.lc+'"><div class="lbin"><span class="lt">'+r.l+(r.fx?'<span class="fx">'+r.fx+'</span>':'')+'</span>'
    +(r.go?'<button type="button" class="eb" data-go="'+r.go+'" aria-label="'+r.l+' 명세 입력" title="명세 입력">'+ICON_EDIT+'</button>':'')+'</div></td>';
  h+='<td class="no">'+(r.n||'')+'</td>';
  h+=tcell(r.a,'a'+k,nm+'금액');
  h+='<td class="rt'+(r.mark?' mk':'')+'">'+(r.mark||r.r||'')+'</td>';
  h+=tcell(r.t,'t'+k,nm+'세액');
  return h+'</tr>';
}
$('#ftBody').innerHTML=FORM.map(fRow).join('');

/* ---------- sub-schedule dialogs (grid rows) ---------- */
function cell(kind,key,label){
  if(kind==='off')return '<div class="c offc" data-l="'+label+'"><div class="cell off" aria-hidden="true"></div></div>';
  if(kind==='in')return '<div class="c" data-l="'+label+'"><input class="cell in" data-edit data-k="'+key+'" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" readonly aria-label="'+label+'"></div>';
  return '<div class="c" data-l="'+label+'"><input class="cell" data-k="'+key+'" readonly tabindex="-1" aria-label="'+label+'"></div>';
}
function rowHTML(r){
  if(r.sub)return '<div class="sub">'+r.sub+'</div>';
  const k=r.k!==undefined?r.k:r.n;
  const lab='<div class="lbl"><span class="lt">'+r.l+'</span>'
    +(r.tag?'<span class="tag">'+r.tag+'</span>':'')
    +(r.note?'<span class="note">'+r.note+'</span>':'')+'</div>';
  return '<div class="row'+(r.total?' total':'')+'"><span class="n">'+(r.n?'('+r.n+')':'')+'</span>'+lab
    +cell(r.a,'a'+k,'금액')
    +'<div class="rt2" data-l="세율">'+(r.r||'')+'</div>'
    +cell(r.t,'t'+k,'세액')+'</div>';
}

/* tab 3 */
function t2row(n,kind){
  const txt=(k,ph)=>'<div class="c" data-l="'+ph+'"><input class="txt" data-edit data-tx="'+k+'" aria-label="'+ph+'" readonly></div>';
  return '<div class="row t2"><span class="n">('+n+')</span>'+txt('b'+n,'업태')+txt('s'+n,'종목')+(kind==='base'?txt('p'+n,'생산요소'):'<div class="c offc" data-l=""></div>')+txt('c'+n,'업종코드')+cell('in','a'+n,'금액')+'</div>';
}
function amtRow(n,l,kind,total,key){
  return '<div class="row t2'+(total?' total':'')+'"><span class="n">('+n+')</span><div class="lbl" style="grid-column:2/5"><span class="lt">'+l+'</span>'+(total&&key?'<span class="pill" id="'+key+'"></span>':'')+'</div><div class="c offc"></div>'+cell(kind,'a'+n,'금액')+'</div>';
}
$('#rowsBase').innerHTML=t2row(31,'base')+t2row(32,'base')+t2row(33,'base')+amtRow(34,'수입금액 제외','in')+amtRow(35,'합계','calc',1,'baseTotalPill');

function parts(total,n,seed){
  const w=[];let ws=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=1+((i*7+seed)%5)/8;w.push(x);ws+=x}
  const out=[];let acc=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n-1;i++){const v=Math.round(total*w[i]/ws/1000)*1000;out.push(v);acc+=v}
  out.push(total-acc);return out;
}
const MDAYS=[31,28,31,30,31,30];
const p2=x=>String(x).padStart(2,'0');
function details(row){
  const a=parts(row.a,row.cnt,row.cnt),tx=[];let acc=0;
  a.forEach((v,i)=>{if(i<a.length-1){const t=Math.floor(v/10);tx.push(t);acc+=t}else tx.push(row.t-acc)});
  const list=a.map((v,i)=>{
    let d;
    if(row.mode==='mon')d='2026-'+p2(i+1)+'-'+MDAYS[i];
    else d='2026-'+p2(1+Math.floor(i*6/row.cnt))+'-'+p2(3+(i*5)%24);
    return {d:d,p:row.pt[i%row.pt.length],i:row.it[i%row.it.length],a:v,t:tx[i]};
  });
  return list.sort((x,y)=>x.d<y.d?-1:x.d>y.d?1:0);
}

let selN=1,selSl=0;

function emptyHTML(){return '<div class="empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="30" height="30" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h8l4 4v14H6zM14 3v4h4M9 13h6M9 17h4"/></svg><span>데이터가 없습니다.</span></div>'}
function cls(v){return v!==0?' bad':''}
function renderPanels(){
  const n=selN,m=MAP[n]||{sl:[],fm:[]};
  $('#selLabel').textContent=LBL[n]?'('+n+') '+LBL[n]:'';
  /* 전표내역 */
  const sl=m.sl||[];
  if(!sl.length){$('#slBody').innerHTML=emptyHTML();$('#sdBody').innerHTML=emptyHTML();$('#sdLabel').textContent=''}
  else{
    if(selSl>=sl.length)selSl=0;
    let sc=0,sa=0,st=0;
    const body=sl.map((r,i)=>{sc+=r.cnt;sa+=r.a;st+=r.t;
      return '<tr data-i="'+i+'"'+(i===selSl?' class="sel"':'')+'><td>'+r.src+'</td><td>'+r.desc+'</td><td class="c">'+r.type+'</td><td class="r">'+r.cnt+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.t)+'</td></tr>'}).join('');
    let foot='<tr><td colspan="3">합계</td><td class="r">'+sc+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(sa)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(st)+'</td></tr>';
    if(m.slCmp!==false){const da=g('a'+n)-sa,dt=g('t'+n)-st;foot+='<tr><td colspan="4">신고서와의 차이</td><td class="r'+cls(da)+'">'+fmt(da)+'</td><td class="r'+cls(dt)+'">'+fmt(dt)+'</td></tr>'}
    $('#slBody').innerHTML='<div class="dscroll"><table class="dtable"><thead><tr><th>전표입력</th><th>상세</th><th class="c">유형</th><th class="r">건수</th><th class="r">금액</th><th class="r">세액</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot>'+foot+'</tfoot></table></div>';
    /* 전표상세내역 */
    const det=details(sl[selSl]);let da2=0,dt2=0;
    $('#sdLabel').textContent=sl[selSl].desc+' · '+sl[selSl].type;
    $('#sdBody').innerHTML='<div class="dscroll"><table class="dtable"><thead><tr><th>날짜</th><th>거래처명</th><th>품명</th><th class="r">금액</th><th class="r">세액</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
      +det.map(d=>{da2+=d.a;dt2+=d.t;return '<tr><td class="num">'+d.d+'</td><td>'+d.p+'</td><td>'+d.i+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(d.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(d.t)+'</td></tr>'}).join('')
      +'</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="3">합계</td><td class="r">'+fmt(da2)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(dt2)+'</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  }
  /* 작성서식내역 */
  const fm=m.fm||[];
  if(!fm.length)$('#fmBody').innerHTML=emptyHTML();
  else{
    let fc=0,fa=0,ft=0;
    const body=fm.map(r=>{if(r.main){fc+=r.cnt;fa+=r.a;ft+=r.t}
      return '<tr><td>'+r.nm+'</td><td'+(r.main?' class="mut"':'')+'>'+r.desc+'</td><td class="c">'+r.type+'</td><td class="r">'+r.cnt+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.t)+'</td></tr>'}).join('');
    const da=g('a'+n)-fa,dt=g('t'+n)-ft;
    $('#fmBody').innerHTML='<div class="dscroll"><table class="dtable"><thead><tr><th>서식</th><th>상세</th><th class="c">유형</th><th class="r">건수</th><th class="r">금액</th><th class="r">세액</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="3">합계</td><td class="r">'+fc+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(fa)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(ft)+'</td></tr><tr><td colspan="4">신고서와의 차이</td><td class="r'+cls(da)+'">'+fmt(da)+'</td><td class="r'+cls(dt)+'">'+fmt(dt)+'</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
  }
}
function selectRow(n){
  if(!n||n===selN&&$('#slBody').innerHTML)return;
  selN=n;selSl=0;
  $$('#ftBody tr').forEach(tr=>tr.classList.toggle('sel',+tr.dataset.n===n));
  renderPanels();
}

function paintAtt(){
  let missing=0;
  const head='<div class="att h"><span>번호</span><span>서식</span><span>작성 메뉴</span><span>상태</span></div>';
  $('#attRows').innerHTML=head+ATT.map(x=>{
    const need=x.need();let st;
    if(!need)st='<span class="pill mute">해당없음</span>';
    else if(x.done)st='<span class="pill ok">작성완료</span>';
    else{st='<span class="pill warn">작성필요</span>';missing++}
    return '<div class="att"><span class="n">'+x.n+'</span><span>'+x.t+'</span><span class="menu-name">'+(x.m||'—')+'</span><span>'+st+'</span></div>';
  }).join('');
  const b=$('#attBadge');b.textContent=missing;b.hidden=missing===0;
  return missing;
}

/* ---------- paint ---------- */
let editing=false,closed=false;
function won(n){return fmt(n)+'원'}
function paint(){
  $$('[data-k]').forEach(el=>{
    const v=g(el.dataset.k);
    if(document.activeElement!==el)el.value=fmt(v);
    el.classList.toggle('zero',v===0);
    el.classList.toggle('neg',v<0);
  });
  $$('[data-tx]').forEach(el=>{if(document.activeElement!==el)el.value=TX[el.dataset.tx]||''});
  const refund=V.t30<0;
  const rt=V.a9?((V.a9-V.a18)/V.a9*100):0;
  $('#sMs').textContent=fmt(V.t9);$('#sMi').textContent=fmt(V.t18);$('#sNp').textContent=fmt(V.tC);
  $('#sRatio').textContent=rt.toFixed(2);$('#fRatio').textContent=rt.toFixed(2);
  $('#cxK').textContent=refund?'환급받을 세액':'납부할 세액';
  $('#cxFinal').textContent=won(Math.abs(V.t30));
  const diff=V.a9-V.a35;
  const bp=$('#baseTotalPill'),bc=$('#baseCheck');
  if(diff===0){bp.textContent='(9)와 일치';bp.className='pill ok';bc.innerHTML='<span class="pill ok">합계 일치</span>'}
  else{bp.textContent='(9)와 '+fmt(diff)+' 차이';bp.className='pill bad';bc.innerHTML='<span class="pill bad">합계 불일치</span>'}
  paintAtt();
  renderPanels();
  lockRefund();
}
function lockRefund(){
  const refund=V.t30<0;
  $$('[data-refund]').forEach(el=>el.disabled=!(editing&&refund));
  $('#refundCard').classList.toggle('dim',!refund);
  $('#refundNote').textContent=refund?won(Math.abs(V.t30))+'을 환급받을 계좌를 입력합니다.':'환급받을 세액이 있을 때 입력합니다.';
}
function lockRecip(){
  const on=editing&&($('input[name="recip"]:checked')||{}).value==='y';
  $$('[data-recip]').forEach(el=>el.disabled=!on);
}
function setEditing(on){
  editing=on;
  document.body.classList.toggle('editing',on);
  $$('[data-edit]').forEach(el=>{
    if(el.type==='radio'||el.type==='checkbox'||el.tagName==='SELECT')el.disabled=!on;
    else el.readOnly=!on;
  });
  $('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';
  $('#modeChip').classList.toggle('on',on);
  lockRefund();lockRecip();
}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
function toast(msg){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},2200)}
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('input[data-k]')&&el.classList.contains('in')){
    el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9\-,]/g,'');
    V[el.dataset.k]=parse(el.value);calc();paint();
  }else if(el.matches('[data-tx]')){TX[el.dataset.tx]=el.value}
  else if(el.id==='rfAcct'){paint()}
});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('input.cell.in')&&editing)setTimeout(()=>el.select(),0);
  const tr=el.closest&&el.closest('#ftBody tr[data-n]');if(tr)selectRow(+tr.dataset.n);
});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const el=e.target;if(el.matches('input[data-k]')){el.value=fmt(g(el.dataset.k))}});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.matches('input.cell.in')&&editing){
    const scope=!$('#bizModal').hidden?$('#bizModal'):!$('#baseModal').hidden?$('#baseModal'):!$('#schedModal').hidden?$('#schedModal'):$('.panel:not([hidden])');
    const list=$$('input.cell.in',scope).filter(x=>x.offsetParent!==null);
    const i=list.indexOf(e.target);if(i>-1&&list[i+1]){e.preventDefault();list[i+1].focus()}
  }
  if(e.key==='Escape'){var _x=document.getElementById('cxModal');if(_x)_x.hidden=true;var _c=document.getElementById('clModal');if(_c)_c.hidden=true;$('#htModal').hidden=true;$('#schedModal').hidden=true;$('#baseModal').hidden=true;$('#attModal').hidden=true;$('#bizModal').hidden=true;$('#rpModal').hidden=true}
});
$$('input[name="recip"]').forEach(r=>r.addEventListener('change',lockRecip));

function showTab(id){
  $$('.tab[data-tab]').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-selected',String(b.dataset.tab===id)));
  $$('.panel').forEach(p=>p.hidden=p.id!==id);
}
$$('.tab[data-tab]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>showTab(b.dataset.tab)));

$('#baseBtn').addEventListener('click',openBase);
let RPT=[
 {f:'2026-01',t:'2026-03',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:0},
 {f:'2026-01',t:'2026-03',k:'수정신고',n:1,c:0},

 {f:'2026-04',t:'2026-06',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:0},
 {f:'2026-07',t:'2026-09',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:0}
],rpCur=-1;
const ym=x=>x.slice(0,4)+'년'+x.slice(5)+'월';
function rpRender(){
  $('#rpBody').innerHTML=RPT.map((r,i)=>'<tr data-i="'+i+'"'+(i===rpCur?' class="sel"':'')+'><td class="c"><input type="checkbox" class="pick" data-i="'+i+'" aria-label="삭제 선택"></td><td class="c">'+ym(r.f)+'</td><td class="c">'+ym(r.t)+'</td><td class="c">'+r.k+'</td><td class="c">'+(r.n?r.n+'차':'')+'</td><td class="c">'+(r.c?'마감':'미마감')+'</td><td class="c">일반과세</td></tr>').join('');
}
function rpOpen(){rpCur=-1;rpRender();$('#rpModal').hidden=false;$('#rpOk').focus()}
function rpConfirm(){
  if(rpCur<0){toast('조회할 신고서를 선택하세요');return}
  const r=RPT[rpCur],y=+r.t.slice(0,4),m=+r.t.slice(5);
  $('#dFrom').value=r.f.slice(0,4)+'.'+r.f.slice(5)+'.01';
  $('#dTo').value=r.t.slice(0,4)+'.'+r.t.slice(5)+'.'+p2(new Date(y,m,0).getDate());
  $('#rptKind').value=r.k==='수정신고'?'2':'1';
  const sq=$('#rptSeq');sq.disabled=r.k!=='수정신고';if(r.n)sq.value=String(r.n);
  $('#rpModal').hidden=true;toast('선택한 신고서를 조회했습니다');
}
$('#rpBtn').addEventListener('click',rpOpen);

$('#rpModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='rpModal')$('#rpModal').hidden=true});
$('#rpOk').addEventListener('click',rpConfirm);
$('#rpBody').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('pick'))return;
  const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-i]');if(!tr)return;
  rpCur=+tr.dataset.i;$$('#rpBody tr').forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle('sel',+t.dataset.i===rpCur));
});
$('#rpBody').addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-i]');if(tr){rpCur=+tr.dataset.i;rpConfirm()}});
$('#rpDel').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const ids=$$('#rpBody .pick:checked').map(x=>+x.dataset.i);
  if(!ids.length){toast('삭제할 신고서를 선택하세요');return}
  if(closed){toast('마감된 상태에서는 삭제할 수 없습니다');return}
  RPT=RPT.filter((_,i)=>!ids.includes(i));rpCur=-1;rpRender();toast('선택한 신고서를 삭제했습니다');
});

$('#baseModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='baseModal')$('#baseModal').hidden=true});

/* row selection + panel selection + sub-schedule dialogs */
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const eb=e.target.closest('.eb');
  if(eb){
    const key=eb.dataset.go;
    $$('[data-sch]').forEach(s=>s.hidden=s.dataset.sch!==key);
    $('#schedTitle').textContent=SCHED_TITLE[key];
    $('#schedModal').hidden=false;$('#schedClose').focus();
    return;
  }
  const tr=e.target.closest('#ftBody tr[data-n]');
  if(tr){selectRow(+tr.dataset.n);return}
  const sr=e.target.closest('#slBody tr[data-i]');
  if(sr){selSl=+sr.dataset.i;renderPanels()}
});

/* header buttons */
function setClosed(on){
  closed=on;setEditing(!on);
  $('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));
  $('#closeTxt').textContent=on?'마감취소':'마감';
  ['delBtn','saveBtn'].forEach(id=>{$('#'+id).disabled=on});
  try{parent.postMessage({type:'vat-closed',screen:'gen',closed:on},'*')}catch(e){}
}
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const d=e.data;if(d&&d.type==='vat-set-closed'&&!!d.closed!==closed)setClosed(!!d.closed)});
$('#closeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(closed){openCx();return}
  if(g('a9')!==g('a35')){openBase();toast('과세표준명세 합계가 (9)와 달라 마감할 수 없습니다');return}
  openClose();
});
function openClose(){
  const head='<div class="att h"><span>번호</span><span>서식</span><span>작성 메뉴</span><span>상태</span></div>';
  let miss=0;
  const main='<div class="att"><span class="n">본표</span><span>부가가치세 신고서 (일반과세자)</span><span class="menu-name">신고서 · 제출서류</span><span><span class="pill ok">마감 대상</span></span></div>';
  const rows=ATT.filter(x=>x.need()).map(x=>{
    let st;
    if(x.done)st='<span class="pill ok">작성완료</span>';
    else{st='<span class="pill warn">작성필요</span>';miss++}
    return '<div class="att"><span class="n">'+x.n+'</span><span>'+x.t+'</span><span class="menu-name">'+(x.m||'—')+'</span><span>'+st+'</span></div>';
  }).join('');
  $('#clRows').innerHTML=head+main+rows;
  $('#clNote').textContent=miss?('작성필요 서식이 '+miss+'건 있습니다. 확인 후 마감하세요.'):'해당 서식이 모두 작성되었습니다.';
  $('#clModal').hidden=false;$('#clGo').focus();
}
$('#clCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#clModal').hidden=true});
$('#clModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='clModal')$('#clModal').hidden=true});
$('#clGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#clModal').hidden=true;snapCL();setClosed(true);toast('마감되었습니다')});
const AMT={'1':()=>g('a1')+g('a5'),'2':()=>g('a10')+g('a12')+g('a11'),'6':()=>g('a17'),'8':()=>g('a89'),'9':()=>g('a44')+g('a45'),'16':()=>g('a12')+g('a45'),'17':()=>g('a46'),'추가':()=>g('a20')};
let CL=[];
function snapCL(){
  const fr=$('#dFrom').value,to=$('#dTo').value;
  const per=fr.slice(5,7)+'-'+to.slice(5,7);
  CL=[{main:1,t:'일반과세자 부가가치세 신고서',per:per,amt:''}].concat(ATT.filter(x=>x.need()).map(x=>({main:0,t:x.t,per:per,amt:(AMT[x.n]?AMT[x.n]():null)})));
}
function drawCx(){
  const head='<div class="cxr h"><span><input type="checkbox" class="pick" id="cxAllCk" aria-label="전체선택(부속서류)"></span><span>서식명</span><span>마감일자</span><span class="r">마감금액</span></div>';
  $('#cxRows').innerHTML=head+CL.map((x,i)=>'<div class="cxr'+(x.main?' main':'')+'"><span>'+(x.main?'<input type="checkbox" class="pick mainck" disabled aria-label="신고서(선택 불가)">':'<input type="checkbox" class="pick" data-i="'+i+'" aria-label="'+x.t+'">')+'</span><span>'+x.t+(x.main?'<span class="tag2">전체마감취소에서만 취소</span>':'')+'</span><span>'+x.per+'</span><span class="r">'+(x.amt?fmt(x.amt):'')+'</span></div>').join('');
}
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.id==='cxAllCk'){$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]').forEach(c=>c.checked=t.checked)}
  else if(t.matches&&t.matches('#cxRows .pick[data-i]')){const a=$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]');const all=$('#cxAllCk');if(all)all.checked=a.length>0&&a.every(c=>c.checked)}
});
function openCx(){if(!CL.length)snapCL();drawCx();$('#cxModal').hidden=false;$('#cxAll').focus()}
$('#cxCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#cxModal').hidden=true});
$('#cxModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='cxModal')$('#cxModal').hidden=true});
$('#cxAll').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#cxModal').hidden=true;CL=[];setClosed(false);toast('전체 마감을 취소했습니다')});
$('#cxSel').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const ids=$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]:checked').map(c=>+c.dataset.i);
  if(!ids.length){toast('취소할 부속서류를 선택하세요');return}
  CL=CL.filter((x,i)=>!ids.includes(i));drawCx();toast('선택한 부속서류 '+ids.length+'건의 마감을 취소했습니다(신고서는 마감 유지)');
});


$('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
$('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#htModal').hidden=false;$('#htGo').focus()});
$('#htClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#htModal').hidden=true});

function htMark(){$$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{const v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;tr.classList.toggle('nz',v>0)})}
htMark();
$('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const y=$('#htYear').value,t=$('#htTerm').value;
  $('#htPer').textContent=t==='1'?y+'0101-'+y+'0630':y+'0701-'+y+'1231';
  $('#htRes').hidden=false;
  $$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{const v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;tr.classList.toggle('nz',v>0)});
  const rf=$('#htReflect');if(rf)rf.disabled=false;
  toast('다시 조회했습니다');
});

$('#htReflect').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(typeof closed!=='undefined'&&closed){toast('마감 상태에서는 반영할 수 없습니다');return}
  const done=[];
  $$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{
    const n=+tr.dataset.n,v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;
    if([23,24,27,28].includes(n)&&v>0){V['t'+n]=v;done.push('('+n+') '+LBL[n]+' '+fmt(v))}
  });
  if(!done.length){toast('반영할 해당사항이 없습니다');return}
  calc();paint();$('#htModal').hidden=true;
  toast('신고서에 반영했습니다: '+done.join(' · '),4200);
});
$('#htModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='htModal')$('#htModal').hidden=true});

/* theme (dark is the default, same key as the other screens) */
function applyTheme(t){document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',t)}
let theme='dark';try{theme=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
applyTheme(theme);
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{theme=theme==='dark'?'light':'dark';applyTheme(theme);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',theme)}catch(e){}});

setEditing(true);
selN=0;selectRow(1);
paint();
</script>

function tcell(kind,key,label){
  if(kind==='off')return '<td class="offc"></td>';
  if(kind==='in')return '<td><input class="cell in" data-edit data-k="'+key+'" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" readonly aria-label="'+label+'"></td>';
  return '<td><input class="cell" data-k="'+key+'" readonly tabindex="-1" aria-label="'+label+'"></td>';
}
function fRow(r){
  const k=r.k!==undefined?r.k:r.n;
  const nm=r.n?'('+r.n+') ':'';
  let h='<tr'+(r.n?' data-n="'+r.n+'"':'')+' class="'+(r.total?'total ':'')+(r.grand?'grand':'')+'">';
  if(r.g1)h+='<th class="g1" rowspan="'+r.g1[1]+'" scope="rowgroup"><span>'+r.g1[0]+'</span></th>';
  if(r.g2)h+='<th class="g2" rowspan="'+r.g2[1]+'" scope="rowgroup"><span>'+r.g2[0]+'</span></th>';
  let lab;
  if(r.sel)lab='<select class="txt sel" data-b="'+r.n+'" data-edit aria-label="'+nm+'업종">'+optsHTML+'</select>';
  else if(r.selOff)lab='<select class="txt sel" disabled aria-label="'+nm+'업종"><option></option></select>';
  else lab='<span class="lt">'+r.l+(r.fx?'<span class="fx">'+r.fx+'</span>':'')+'</span>';
  h+='<td class="lb" colspan="'+r.lc+'"><div class="lbin">'+lab
    +(r.go?'<button type="button" class="eb" data-go="1" aria-label="가산세 명세 입력" title="가산세 명세 입력">'+ICON_EDIT+'</button>':'')+'</div></td>';
  h+='<td class="no">'+(r.n||'')+'</td>';
  h+=tcell(r.a,'a'+k,nm+'금액');
  h+='<td class="rt" id="rt1_'+(r.n||'')+'">'+(r.ref?'<button type="button" class="lnk" data-ref="1">[참고]</button>':'')+'</td>';
  h+='<td class="rt'+(r.mark?' mk':'')+'">'+(r.mark||r.r2||'')+'</td>';
  h+=tcell(r.t,'t'+k,nm+'세액');
  return h+'</tr>';
}

  return '<div class="c" data-l="'+label+'"><input class="cell" data-k="'+key+'" readonly tabindex="-1" aria-label="'+label+'"></div>';
}

}

function taxOf(n,a){
  if(n===5)return Math.floor(a*bizRate(5)*0.1);
  if(n===15)return Math.floor(a*0.005);
  if(n===22)return Math.min(Math.floor(a*(CRATE[CR]||0.013)),LIMIT22);
  return 0;
}

function emptyHTML(){return '<div class="empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="30" height="30" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h8l4 4v14H6zM14 3v4h4M9 13h6M9 17h4"/></svg><span>데이터가 없습니다.</span></div>'}
function cls(v){return v!==0?' bad':''}
function panelTable(first,rows,n){
  let c=0,a=0,t=0;
  const body=rows.map(r=>{const tx=taxOf(n,r.a);c+=r.cnt;a+=r.a;t+=tx;
    return '<tr><td>'+r.x1+'</td><td>'+r.x2+'</td><td class="c">'+r.type+'</td><td class="r">'+r.cnt+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(tx)+'</td></tr>'}).join('');
  const da=g('a'+n)-a,dt=g('t'+n)-t;
  return '<div class="dscroll" style="max-height:none;height:210px"><table class="dtable"><thead><tr><th>'+first+'</th><th>상세</th><th class="c">유형</th><th class="r">건수</th><th class="r">금액</th><th class="r">세액</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="3">합계</td><td class="r">'+c+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(t)+'</td></tr><tr><td colspan="4">신고서와의 차이</td><td class="r'+cls(da)+'">'+fmt(da)+'</td><td class="r'+cls(dt)+'">'+fmt(dt)+'</td></tr></tfoot></table></div>';
}
function renderPanels(){
  const n=selN,m=MAP[n]||{sl:[],fm:[]};
  $('#selLabel').textContent=LBL[n]?'('+n+') '+LBL[n]:'';
  $('#slBody').innerHTML=m.sl.length?panelTable('전표입력',m.sl.map(r=>({x1:r.src,x2:r.desc,type:r.type,cnt:r.cnt,a:r.a})),n):emptyHTML();
  $('#fmBody').innerHTML=m.fm.length?panelTable('작성서식',m.fm.map(r=>({x1:r.nm,x2:r.desc,type:r.type,cnt:r.cnt,a:r.a})),n):emptyHTML();
}
function selectRow(n){
  if(!n)return;
  selN=n;
  $$('#ftBody tr').forEach(tr=>tr.classList.toggle('sel',+tr.dataset.n===n));
  renderPanels();
}

 {n:'7',t:'의제매입세액 공제신고서',m:'의제류공제',need:()=>g('t16')>0,done:0},
 {n:'8',t:'그 밖에 필요한 증명서류',m:'',need:()=>false,done:0}
];
function paintAtt(){
  let missing=0;
  const head='<div class="att h"><span>번호</span><span>서식</span><span>작성 메뉴</span><span>상태</span></div>';
  $('#attRows').innerHTML=head+ATT.map(x=>{
    const need=x.need();let st;
    if(!need)st='<span class="pill mute">해당없음</span>';
    else if(x.done)st='<span class="pill ok">작성완료</span>';
    else{st='<span class="pill warn">작성필요</span>';missing++}
    return '<div class="att"><span class="n">'+x.n+'</span><span>'+x.t+'</span><span class="menu-name">'+(x.m||'—')+'</span><span>'+st+'</span></div>';
  }).join('');
  const b=$('#attBadge');b.textContent=missing;b.hidden=missing===0;
}

/* ---------- paint ---------- */
let editing=false,closed=false;
function paint(){
  $$('[data-k]').forEach(el=>{
    const v=g(el.dataset.k);
    if(document.activeElement!==el)el.value=fmt(v);
    el.classList.toggle('zero',v===0);
    el.classList.toggle('neg',v<0);
  });
  $$('[data-tx]').forEach(el=>{if(document.activeElement!==el)el.value=TX[el.dataset.tx]||''});
  $$('select[data-b]').forEach(el=>{el.value=String(g('b'+el.dataset.b))});
  for(let n=5;n<=9;n++){const r=bizRate(n);$('#rt1_'+n).textContent=r?Math.round(r*100)+'/100':''}
  const exempt=FINAL&&V.a13<48000000;
  $('#sMs').textContent=fmt(V.t13);
  $('#sMi').textContent=fmt(V.t24);
  $('#sNp').textContent=fmt(V.t13-V.t24);
  $('#sFinal').textContent=fmt(V.tR);
  $('#cxK').textContent=exempt?'납부의무 면제':'실제 납부세액';
  $('#cxFinal').textContent=won(V.tR);
  const diff=V.a13-V.a35;
  const bp=$('#baseTotalPill'),bc=$('#baseCheck');
  if(diff===0){bp.textContent='합계와 일치';bp.className='pill ok';bc.innerHTML='<span class="pill ok">합계 일치</span>'}
  else{bp.textContent='합계와 '+fmt(diff)+' 차이';bp.className='pill bad';bc.innerHTML='<span class="pill bad">합계 불일치</span>'}
  paintAtt();
  renderPanels();
}
function lockRecip(){
  const on=editing&&($('input[name="recip"]:checked')||{}).value==='y';
  $$('[data-recip]').forEach(el=>el.disabled=!on);
}
function setEditing(on){
  editing=on;
  document.body.classList.toggle('editing',on);
  $$('[data-edit]').forEach(el=>{
    if(el.type==='radio'||el.type==='checkbox'||el.tagName==='SELECT')el.disabled=!on;
    else el.readOnly=!on;
  });
  $('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';
  $('#modeChip').classList.toggle('on',on);
  lockRecip();
}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
function toast(msg,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('input[data-k]')&&el.classList.contains('in')){
    el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9\-,]/g,'');
    V[el.dataset.k]=parse(el.value);calc();paint();
  }else if(el.matches('[data-tx]')){TX[el.dataset.tx]=el.value}
});
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('select[data-b]')){V['b'+el.dataset.b]=+el.value;calc();paint()}
});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('input.cell.in')&&editing)setTimeout(()=>el.select(),0);
  const tr=el.closest&&el.closest('#ftBody tr[data-n]');if(tr)selectRow(+tr.dataset.n);
});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const el=e.target;if(el.matches('input[data-k]')){el.value=fmt(g(el.dataset.k))}});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.matches('input.cell.in')&&editing){
    const modal=$$('.scrim').find(m=>!m.hidden);
    const scope=modal||$('.panel');
    const list=$$('input.cell.in',scope).filter(x=>x.offsetParent!==null);
    const i=list.indexOf(e.target);if(i>-1&&list[i+1]){e.preventDefault();list[i+1].focus()}
  }
  if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true);
});
$$('input[name="recip"]').forEach(r=>r.addEventListener('change',lockRecip));

  if(e.target.closest('.eb')){$('#schedModal').hidden=false;$('#schedClose').focus();return}
  if(e.target.closest('[data-ref]')){toast('신용카드매출전표등 발행세액공제: 발행금액의 1.3%, 연 1,000만원 한도 (2026.12.31.까지)',4200);return}
  const tr=e.target.closest('#ftBody tr[data-n]');
  if(tr)selectRow(+tr.dataset.n);
});
function bindModal(id,openBtn,closeBtn,onOpen){
  const m=$('#'+id);
  if(openBtn)$('#'+openBtn).addEventListener('click',()=>{if(onOpen)onOpen();m.hidden=false;const f=$('#'+closeBtn);if(f)f.focus()});
  if(closeBtn)$('#'+closeBtn).addEventListener('click',()=>{m.hidden=true});
  m.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===m)m.hidden=true});
}
bindModal('schedModal',null,'schedClose');
bindModal('baseModal','baseBtn','baseClose');
bindModal('attModal','attBtn','attClose');
bindModal('bizModal','bizBtn','bizClose');
bindModal('htModal','htBtn','htClose');

function htMark(){$$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{const v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;tr.classList.toggle('nz',v>0)})}
htMark();
$('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const y=$('#htYear').value,t=$('#htTerm').value;

  $('#htRes').hidden=false;
  $$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{const v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;tr.classList.toggle('nz',v>0)});

});

$('#htReflect').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(typeof closed!=='undefined'&&closed){toast('마감 상태에서는 반영할 수 없습니다');return}
  const sm={};
  $$('#htRes tr.ded').forEach(tr=>{const n=+tr.dataset.n,v=+tr.querySelector('[data-v]').dataset.v;sm[n]=(sm[n]||0)+v});
  const done=[];
  [25,26].forEach(n=>{if(sm[n]>0){V['t'+n]=sm[n];done.push('('+n+') '+({25:'매입자 납부특례 기납부세액',26:'예정 부과(신고) 세액'})[n]+' '+fmt(sm[n]))}});
  if(!done.length){toast('반영할 해당사항이 없습니다');return}
  calc();paint();$('#htModal').hidden=true;
  toast('신고서에 반영했습니다: '+done.join(' · '),4200);
});

function fmtDt(el){const d=el.value.replace(/\D/g,'').slice(0,8);el.value=d.length===8?d.slice(0,4)+'.'+d.slice(4,6)+'.'+d.slice(6):el.value}
['dFrom','dTo'].forEach(id=>$('#'+id).addEventListener('blur',e=>fmtDt(e.target)));

/* header buttons */
function setClosed(on){
  closed=on;setEditing(!on);
  $('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));
  $('#closeTxt').textContent=on?'마감취소':'마감';
  ['delBtn','saveBtn'].forEach(id=>{$('#'+id).disabled=on});
}
$('#closeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(closed){openCx();return}
  if(g('a13')!==g('a35')){$('#baseModal').hidden=false;toast('과세표준명세 합계가 공급대가 합계와 달라 마감할 수 없습니다');return}
  openClose();
});
function openClose(){
  const head='<div class="att h"><span>번호</span><span>서식</span><span>작성 메뉴</span><span>상태</span></div>';
  let miss=0;
  const main='<div class="att"><span class="n">본표</span><span>부가가치세 신고서 (간이과세자)</span><span class="menu-name">신고서 · 제출서류</span><span><span class="pill ok">마감 대상</span></span></div>';
  const rows=ATT.filter(x=>x.need()).map(x=>{
    let st;
    if(x.done)st='<span class="pill ok">작성완료</span>';
    else{st='<span class="pill warn">작성필요</span>';miss++}
    return '<div class="att"><span class="n">'+x.n+'</span><span>'+x.t+'</span><span class="menu-name">'+(x.m||'—')+'</span><span>'+st+'</span></div>';
  }).join('');
  $('#clRows').innerHTML=head+main+rows;
  $('#clNote').textContent=miss?('작성필요 서식이 '+miss+'건 있습니다. 확인 후 마감하세요.'):'해당 서식이 모두 작성되었습니다.';
  $('#clModal').hidden=false;$('#clGo').focus();
}
$('#clCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#clModal').hidden=true});
$('#clModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='clModal')$('#clModal').hidden=true});
$('#clGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#clModal').hidden=true;snapCL();setClosed(true);toast('마감되었습니다')});
const AMT={'1':()=>g('a15'),'4':()=>g('a22'),'5':()=>g('a89'),'6':()=>g('a88'),'7':()=>g('t16')};
let CL=[];
function snapCL(){
  const fr=$('#dFrom').value,to=$('#dTo').value;
  const per=fr.slice(5,7)+'-'+to.slice(5,7);
  CL=[{main:1,t:'간이과세자 부가가치세 신고서',per:per,amt:''}].concat(ATT.filter(x=>x.need()).map(x=>({main:0,t:x.t,per:per,amt:(AMT[x.n]?AMT[x.n]():null)})));
}
function drawCx(){
  const head='<div class="cxr h"><span><input type="checkbox" class="pick" id="cxAllCk" aria-label="전체선택(부속서류)"></span><span>서식명</span><span>마감일자</span><span class="r">마감금액</span></div>';
  $('#cxRows').innerHTML=head+CL.map((x,i)=>'<div class="cxr'+(x.main?' main':'')+'"><span>'+(x.main?'<input type="checkbox" class="pick mainck" disabled aria-label="신고서(선택 불가)">':'<input type="checkbox" class="pick" data-i="'+i+'" aria-label="'+x.t+'">')+'</span><span>'+x.t+(x.main?'<span class="tag2">전체마감취소에서만 취소</span>':'')+'</span><span>'+x.per+'</span><span class="r">'+(x.amt?fmt(x.amt):'')+'</span></div>').join('');
}
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.id==='cxAllCk'){$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]').forEach(c=>c.checked=t.checked)}
  else if(t.matches&&t.matches('#cxRows .pick[data-i]')){const a=$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]');const all=$('#cxAllCk');if(all)all.checked=a.length>0&&a.every(c=>c.checked)}
});
function openCx(){if(!CL.length)snapCL();drawCx();$('#cxModal').hidden=false;$('#cxAll').focus()}
$('#cxCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#cxModal').hidden=true});
$('#cxModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target.id==='cxModal')$('#cxModal').hidden=true});
$('#cxAll').addEventListener('click',()=>{$('#cxModal').hidden=true;CL=[];setClosed(false);toast('전체 마감을 취소했습니다')});
$('#cxSel').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const ids=$$('#cxRows .pick[data-i]:checked').map(c=>+c.dataset.i);
  if(!ids.length){toast('취소할 부속서류를 선택하세요');return}
  CL=CL.filter((x,i)=>!ids.includes(i));drawCx();toast('선택한 부속서류 '+ids.length+'건의 마감을 취소했습니다(신고서는 마감 유지)');
});

/* saved-return list */
let RPT=[
 {f:'2024-01',t:'2024-12',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:1},
 {f:'2025-01',t:'2025-12',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:0},
 {f:'2025-01',t:'2025-12',k:'수정신고',n:1,c:0},
 {f:'2026-01',t:'2026-06',k:'정기신고',n:0,c:0}
],rpCur=-1;
const ym=x=>x.slice(0,4)+'년'+x.slice(5)+'월';
function rpRender(){
  $('#rpBody').innerHTML=RPT.map((r,i)=>'<tr data-i="'+i+'"'+(i===rpCur?' class="sel"':'')+'><td class="c"><input type="checkbox" class="pick" data-i="'+i+'" aria-label="삭제 선택"></td><td class="c">'+ym(r.f)+'</td><td class="c">'+ym(r.t)+'</td><td class="c">'+r.k+'</td><td class="c">'+(r.n?r.n+'차':'')+'</td><td class="c">'+(r.c?'마감':'미마감')+'</td><td class="c">간이과세</td></tr>').join('');
}
function rpConfirm(){
  if(rpCur<0){toast('조회할 신고서를 선택하세요');return}
  const r=RPT[rpCur],y=+r.t.slice(0,4),m=+r.t.slice(5);
  $('#dFrom').value=r.f.slice(0,4)+'.'+r.f.slice(5)+'.01';
  $('#dTo').value=r.t.slice(0,4)+'.'+r.t.slice(5)+'.'+p2(new Date(y,m,0).getDate());
  $('#rptKind').value=r.k==='수정신고'?'2':'1';
  const sq=$('#rptSeq');sq.disabled=r.k!=='수정신고';if(r.n)sq.value=String(r.n);
  $('#rpModal').hidden=true;toast('선택한 신고서를 조회했습니다');
}
bindModal('rpModal','rpBtn','rpCancel',()=>{rpCur=-1;rpRender()});
$('#rpOk').addEventListener('click',rpConfirm);
$('#rpBody').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  if(e.target.classList.contains('pick'))return;
  const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-i]');if(!tr)return;
  rpCur=+tr.dataset.i;$$('#rpBody tr').forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle('sel',+t.dataset.i===rpCur));
});
$('#rpBody').addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-i]');if(tr){rpCur=+tr.dataset.i;rpConfirm()}});
$('#rpDel').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  const ids=$$('#rpBody .pick:checked').map(x=>+x.dataset.i);
  if(!ids.length){toast('삭제할 신고서를 선택하세요');return}
  if(closed){toast('마감된 상태에서는 삭제할 수 없습니다');return}
  RPT=RPT.filter((_,i)=>!ids.includes(i));rpCur=-1;rpRender();toast('선택한 신고서를 삭제했습니다');
});

<script>
const CFG={"doc": "세금계산서", "tax": true, "startK": "p", "sNames": ["가온상사", "나래푸드", "다온케이터링", "라온상회", "마루식당", "바른유통", "새봄카페", "아람상사"], "pNames": ["한솔식자재", "대성유통", "푸른닭고기", "제일포장", "우리가스", "늘푸른기름", "알찬소스", "동방주방기기", "청정수산", "햇살농산", "미래물류", "새롬위생", "나눔인쇄", "하늘간판"], "sH1": {"total": 3200000, "R": 16}, "pH1": {"total": 62500000, "R": 33}, "sH2": {"total": 2800000, "R": 14}, "pH2": {"total": 58000000, "R": 30}, "loadMsg": "홈택스 수집 세금계산서를 불러왔습니다", "mis": {"p": {"s": 4300000, "n": 1}}};
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const fmt=n=>(n<0?'-':'')+Math.abs(Math.trunc(n)).toLocaleString('en-US');
const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
const dot=ym=>ym.replace('-','.');

/* ---------- sample data: one record = one invoice, dated by month ---------- */
function parts(total,n,seed){
  if(n<=0)return [];
  const w=[];let ws=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=1+((i*7+seed)%5)/8;w.push(x);ws+=x}
  const out=[];let acc=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n-1;i++){const v=Math.round(total*w[i]/ws/1000)*1000;out.push(v);acc+=v}
  out.push(total-acc);return out;
}
const reg=i=>String(100+(i*37)%800)+'-'+String(10+(i*13)%80)+'-'+String(10000+(i*7919)%89999);
const REPS=['김민수','이서연','박지훈','최유진','정하늘','강도윤','조은채','윤재호','임수아','한지민','오세훈','서가은','권태양','문해린'];
const H1=['2026-01','2026-02','2026-03','2026-04','2026-05','2026-06'];
const H2=['2025-07','2025-08','2025-09','2025-10','2025-11','2025-12'];
const PART={};
const EREC={s:[],p:[]};
['s','p'].forEach((k,ki)=>{
  const names=CFG[k+'Names'];
  PART[k]=names.map((n,i)=>({name:n,reg:reg(i*3+ki*3+2),rep:REPS[(i+ki*3)%REPS.length]}));
  [['H1',H1],['H2',H2]].forEach(([key,months],hi)=>{
    const d=CFG[k+key];if(!d||!d.R)return;
    parts(d.total,d.R,ki*3+hi+2).forEach((v,r)=>{
      EREC[k].push({p:r%names.length,ym:months[(r*5+(r>>1))%months.length],cnt:1,sup:v,tax:CFG.tax?Math.floor(v/10):0});
    });
  });
});
const DATA={s:{xb:[],xr:[]},p:{xb:[],xr:[]}};   /* 전자 외: entered by hand */
let cur={k:CFG.startK,t:'e',id:'b'};
let mode='file',closed=false;
let RANGE={from:'2026-01',to:'2026-06'};
const inR=ym=>ym>=RANGE.from&&ym<=RANGE.to;
const KEY=c=>c.t+c.id;

function view(k,t,id){
  if(t==='e'){
    if(id==='r')return [];
    const m=new Map();
    EREC[k].filter(r=>inR(r.ym)).forEach(r=>{
      const o=m.get(r.p)||{name:PART[k][r.p].name,reg:PART[k][r.p].reg,rep:PART[k][r.p].rep,liq:'',unit:'부',cnt:0,sup:0,tax:0};
      o.cnt+=r.cnt;o.sup+=r.sup;o.tax+=r.tax;m.set(r.p,o);
    });
    return Array.from(m.entries()).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(e=>e[1]);
  }
  const l=DATA[k][t+id];
  return mode==='sub'?l.filter(r=>inR(r.ym)):l;
}
const sum=l=>l.reduce((o,r)=>({c:o.c+1,n:o.n+r.cnt,s:o.s+r.sup,t:o.t+r.tax}),{c:0,n:0,s:0,t:0});
const add=(a,b)=>({c:a.c+b.c,n:a.n+b.n,s:a.s+b.s,t:a.t+b.t});
const sm=(k,t,id)=>sum(view(k,t,id));
const smT=(k,t)=>add(sm(k,t,'b'),sm(k,t,'r'));

/* ---------- summary tables ---------- */
const NC=CFG.tax?6:5;
function vcells(v){return '<td class="r">'+fmt(v.c)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v.n)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v.s)+'</td>'+(CFG.tax?'<td class="r">'+fmt(v.t)+'</td>':'')}
function srow(k,t,id,label,first,T){
  const sel=cur.k===k&&cur.t===t&&cur.id===id;
  return '<tr data-k="'+k+'" data-t="'+t+'" data-id="'+id+'" class="pick-row'+(sel?' sel':'')+'" tabindex="0">'+(first?'<th class="g1" rowspan="3" scope="rowgroup">'+T+'</th>':'')+'<td class="ct">'+label+'</td>'+vcells(sm(k,t,id))+'</tr>';
}
const ssub=(k,t)=>'<tr class="sub"><td class="ct">소계</td>'+vcells(smT(k,t))+'</tr>';
function stable(k){
  const nm=k==='s'?'매출':'매입';
  const tot=add(smT(k,'e'),smT(k,'x'));
  return '<div class="sec '+(k==='s'?'sale':'buy')+'"><h3>'+nm+CFG.doc+'</h3></div>'
   +'<div class="swrap"><table class="stbl"><thead><tr><th>유형</th><th>구분</th><th>'+nm+'처</th><th>매수</th><th>공급가액</th>'+(CFG.tax?'<th>부가세</th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'
   +srow(k,'e','b','사업자',1,'전자')+srow(k,'e','r','주민번호',0)+ssub(k,'e')
   +srow(k,'x','b','사업자',1,'전자 외')+srow(k,'x','r','주민번호',0)+ssub(k,'x')
   +'<tr class="total"><td colspan="2" class="ct">합계</td>'+vcells(tot)+'</tr>'
   +'</tbody></table></div>';
}
let RECON=null;
const nowStr=()=>{const d=new Date(),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return d.getFullYear()+'.'+p(d.getMonth()+1)+'.'+p(d.getDate())+' '+p(d.getHours())+':'+p(d.getMinutes())};
function reconRows(){
  const out=[];
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{
    const e=smT(k,'e'),h=RECON.ht[k];
    const items=[['총 매수',e.n,h.n],['총 합계금액',e.s+e.t,h.s+h.t],['총 공급가액',e.s,h.s]];
    if(CFG.tax)items.push(['총 세액',e.t,h.t]);
    items.forEach((it,i)=>out.push({k,first:i===0,n:items.length,nm:k==='s'?'매출':'매입',lb:it[0],a:it[1],b:it[2]}));
  });
  return out;
}
function reconHtml(){
  const hd='<div class="sec recon"><h3>홈택스 대사 <span class="rsub">전자'+CFG.doc+' · 홈택스 세무대리·납세관리 목록조회 기준</span></h3></div>';
  if(!RECON)return hd+'<p class="rc-empty">[홈택스 자료조회]를 누르면 홈택스 전자'+CFG.doc+' 목록조회 합계와 프로그램에 수집된 전자분 합계를 대사합니다.</p>';
  const rows=reconRows(),bad=rows.filter(r=>r.a!==r.b).length;
  const stale=RECON.from!==RANGE.from||RECON.to!==RANGE.to;
  let h=hd+'<div class="rc-meta"><span>대사일자 <b>'+RECON.at+'</b></span><span>대사기간 <b>'+dot(RECON.from)+' ~ '+dot(RECON.to)+'</b></span><span>대사결과 <b class="'+(bad?'rc-bad':'rc-ok')+'">'+(bad?'불일치 '+bad+'건':'전체 일치')+'</b></span>'+(stale?'<span class="rc-bad">조회기간이 달라졌습니다. 다시 대사하세요.</span>':'')+'</div>'
   +'<div class="swrap"><table class="stbl rtbl"><thead><tr><th>구분</th><th>항목</th><th>프로그램 (전자)</th><th>홈택스</th><th>차이</th><th>결과</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const d=r.a-r.b,ok=d===0;
    h+='<tr>'+(r.first?'<th class="g1" rowspan="'+r.n+'" scope="rowgroup">'+r.nm+'</th>':'')+'<td class="ct">'+r.lb+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.b)+'</td><td class="r'+(ok?'':' rc-bad')+'">'+fmt(d)+'</td><td class="ct"><span class="rc-b '+(ok?'rc-ok':'rc-bad')+'">'+(ok?'일치':'불일치')+'</span></td></tr>';
  });
  h+='</tbody></table></div>';
  if(bad)h+='<p class="rc-note">※ 홈택스 목록조회에는 있으나 프로그램에 수집되지 않은 전자'+CFG.doc+'가 있습니다. [불러오기]로 다시 수집한 뒤 [홈택스 자료조회]로 재대사하세요.</p>';
  return h;
}
function renderSummary(){$('#sumBody').innerHTML=stable('s')+stable('p')+reconHtml()}
/* 홈택스 목록조회 합계(견본): 프로그램 전자분과 같게 두되 CFG.mis 가 있으면 그만큼 다르게 */
function runRecon(quiet){
  const ht={};
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{const e=smT(k,'e'),m=(CFG.mis&&CFG.mis[k])||null;ht[k]={n:e.n+(m?m.n:0),s:e.s+(m?m.s:0),t:e.t+(CFG.tax&&m?Math.floor(m.s/10):0)}});
  RECON={at:nowStr(),from:RANGE.from,to:RANGE.to,ht};
  refresh(false);
  if(quiet)return;
  const bad=reconRows().filter(r=>r.a!==r.b).length;
  toast(bad?'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 불일치 '+bad+'건':'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 전체 일치',2800);
}

/* ---------- detail list ---------- */
function cols(t){
  const c=[{f:'name',l:'거래처명',k:'txt'},{f:'reg',l:cur.id==='b'?'등록번호':'주민등록번호',k:'txt'},{f:'cnt',l:'매수',k:'num'},{f:'sup',l:'공급가액',k:'num'}];
  if(CFG.tax)c.push({f:'tax',l:'부가세',k:'num'});
  c.push({f:'rep',l:'대표자명',k:'txt'});
  if(CFG.tax){c.push({f:'liq',l:'주류업종',k:'txt'});c.push({f:'unit',l:'단위신고',k:'sel'})}
  if(t==='x')c.push({f:'ym',l:'작성월',k:'month'});
  return c;
}
const titleOf=c=>'('+(c.k==='p'?'매입':'매출')+') '+(c.t==='e'?'전자':'전자 외')+' '+CFG.doc+' · '+(c.id==='b'?'사업자':'주민번호');
function detailFoot(){
  const cs=cols(cur.t),v=sm(cur.k,cur.t,cur.id);
  const total=cs.length+1+(cur.t==='x'?1:0);
  const nn=CFG.tax?3:2;
  const rest=total-3-nn;
  const num=x=>{let h='<td class="r">'+fmt(x.n)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(x.s)+'</td>';if(CFG.tax)h+='<td class="r">'+fmt(x.t)+'</td>';return h};
  const e=smT(cur.k,'e');
  $('#dFoot').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="3" class="ct">합계 ('+fmt(v.c)+'건)</td>'+num(v)+'<td colspan="'+rest+'"></td></tr>'
   +'<tr class="f2"><td colspan="3" class="ct">전자신고 마감합계</td>'+num(e)+'<td colspan="'+rest+'" class="hint">'+dot(RANGE.from)+' ~ '+dot(RANGE.to)+'</td></tr>';
}
function renderDetail(){
  const list=view(cur.k,cur.t,cur.id),edit=cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'&&!closed;
  const cs=cols(cur.t);
  $('#dTitle').textContent=titleOf(cur);
  $('#addRow').hidden=!(cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file');
  $('#addRow').disabled=closed;
  const ncol=cs.length+1+(cur.t==='x'?1:0);
  const th='<tr><th style="width:44px">No</th>'+cs.map(c=>'<th>'+c.l+'</th>').join('')+(cur.t==='x'?'<th style="width:40px"></th>':'')+'</tr>';
  let body;
  if(!list.length){
    body='<tr><td colspan="'+ncol+'"><div class="empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="30" height="30" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h8l4 4v14H6zM14 3v4h4M9 13h6M9 17h4"/></svg><span>'+(cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'?'입력된 내용이 없습니다. [+ 행 추가]로 입력하세요.':'데이터가 없습니다.')+'</span></div></td></tr>';
  }else body=list.map((r,i)=>{
    let h='<tr data-i="'+i+'"><td class="ct n">'+(i+1)+'</td>';
    cs.forEach(c=>{
      const val=r[c.f];
      if(!edit){
        if(c.k==='num')h+='<td class="r">'+fmt(val)+'</td>';
        else if(c.f==='reg')h+='<td class="ct num">'+esc(val)+'</td>';
        else if(c.f==='unit'||c.f==='ym')h+='<td class="ct">'+esc(c.f==='ym'?dot(val):val)+'</td>';
        else h+='<td>'+esc(val)+'</td>';
      }else if(c.k==='num')h+='<td><input class="cell in" data-f="'+c.f+'" inputmode="numeric" value="'+fmt(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
      else if(c.k==='sel')h+='<td><select class="txt" data-f="unit" aria-label="'+c.l+'"><option'+(val==='여'?' selected':'')+'>여</option><option'+(val==='부'?' selected':'')+'>부</option></select></td>';
      else if(c.k==='month')h+='<td><input class="txt" type="month" data-f="ym" value="'+esc(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
      else h+='<td><input class="txt'+(c.f==='reg'?' num':'')+'" data-f="'+c.f+'" value="'+esc(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
    });
    if(cur.t==='x')h+=edit?'<td class="ct"><button type="button" class="eb" data-del="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제" title="행 삭제"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="12" height="12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3.5 3.5l9 9M12.5 3.5l-9 9"/></svg></button></td>':'<td></td>';
    return h+'</tr>';
  }).join('')+'<tr class="fill"><td colspan="'+ncol+'"></td></tr>';
  $('#dWrap').innerHTML='<table class="dtbl"><thead>'+th+'</thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot id="dFoot"></tfoot></table>';
  detailFoot();
}

/* ---------- side totals ---------- */
function renderAside(){
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{
    const e=smT(k,'e'),x=smT(k,'x');
    $(k==='s'?'#asS':'#asP').innerHTML=
      '<div class="sumline"><span>전자</span><span class="num">'+fmt(e.s)+'</span></div>'
     +'<div class="sumline"><span>전자 외</span><span class="num">'+fmt(x.s)+'</span></div>'
     +'<div class="sumline"><span><b>합계</b></span><span class="num"><b>'+fmt(e.s+x.s)+'</b></span></div>';
  });
}
function refresh(full){renderSummary();renderAside();if(full)renderDetail();else detailFoot()}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
function toast(msg,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function pickRow(tr){cur={k:tr.dataset.k,t:tr.dataset.t,id:tr.dataset.id};refresh(true)}
const editable=()=>cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'&&!closed;
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const tr=e.target.closest('tr.pick-row');if(tr){pickRow(tr);return}
  const d=e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if(d&&editable()){DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)].splice(+d.dataset.del,1);refresh(true)}
});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  const tr=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('tr.pick-row');
  if(tr&&(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' ')){e.preventDefault();pickRow(tr)}
  if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true);
  if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.matches('#dWrap input')){
    const list=$$('#dWrap input').filter(x=>x.offsetParent!==null),i=list.indexOf(e.target);
    if(i>-1&&list[i+1]){e.preventDefault();list[i+1].focus()}
  }
});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const el=e.target;if(!el.matches('#dWrap [data-f]')||!editable())return;
  const tr=el.closest('tr'),r=DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i],f=el.dataset.f;
  if(['cnt','sup','tax'].includes(f)){
    el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');
    r[f]=parse(el.value);
    if(CFG.tax){
      if(f==='sup'&&r.auto!==false){r.tax=Math.floor(r.sup/10);tr.querySelector('[data-f="tax"]').value=fmt(r.tax)}
      if(f==='tax')r.auto=false;
    }
    refresh(false);
  }else r[f]=el.value;
});
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('#dWrap [data-f]')&&editable()){const tr=el.closest('tr');DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i][el.dataset.f]=el.value}
});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches('#dWrap input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('#dWrap input.cell')&&editable()){const tr=el.closest('tr');el.value=fmt(DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i][el.dataset.f])}
});
$('#addRow').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(!editable())return;
  DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)].push({name:'',reg:'',cnt:0,sup:0,tax:0,rep:'',liq:'',unit:'부',ym:RANGE.to});
  refresh(true);
  const rows=$$('#dWrap tbody tr');const last=rows[rows.length-1];
  if(last){last.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});const f=last.querySelector('input');if(f)f.focus()}
});

/* ---------- period / lookup mode ---------- */
function applyPeriod(){
  const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];
  RANGE={from:o.dataset.from.replace('.','-'),to:o.dataset.to.replace('.','-')};
  $('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;
}
function syncButtons(){
  const sub=mode==='sub',lock=sub||closed;
  ['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(id=>{$('#'+id).disabled=lock});
  $('#closeBtn').disabled=sub;
  $('#addRow').disabled=lock;
  $('#rptDate').disabled=lock;
  $('#modeChip').textContent=sub?'제출용 조회':(closed?'마감':'작성 중');
  $('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';
}
function setMode(m){
  mode=m;const sub=m==='sub';
  $('#subBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(sub));
  $('#periodSel').disabled=sub;
  const f=$('#dFrom'),t=$('#dTo');
  f.type=sub?'month':'text';t.type=sub?'month':'text';
  f.readOnly=!sub;t.readOnly=!sub;
  f.style.width=t.style.width=sub?'140px':'80px';
  if(sub){f.value=RANGE.from;t.value=RANGE.to}else applyPeriod();
  $('#dueBox').hidden=sub;$('#subHint').hidden=!sub;
  syncButtons();refresh(true);
}
function runQuery(){
  if(mode==='sub'){
    const a=$('#dFrom').value,b=$('#dTo').value;
    if(!a||!b){toast('조회 기간을 선택하세요');return}
    if(a>b){toast('시작월이 종료월보다 늦습니다');return}
    RANGE={from:a,to:b};
  }
  refresh(true);toast('조회했습니다');
}
const sm_=$('#subModal');
function openSub(){$('#sbFrom').value=RANGE.from;$('#sbTo').value=RANGE.to;sm_.hidden=false;$('#sbFrom').focus()}
function closeSub(){sm_.hidden=true}
$('#subBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(mode==='sub')setMode('file');else openSub()});
$('#subX').addEventListener('click',closeSub);$('#subCancel').addEventListener('click',closeSub);
sm_.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===sm_)closeSub()});
function subRange(){
  const a=$('#sbFrom').value,b=$('#sbTo').value;
  if(!a||!b){toast('조회 기간을 선택하세요');return false}
  if(a>b){toast('시작월이 종료월보다 늦습니다');return false}
  RANGE={from:a,to:b};return true;
}
$('#subGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!subRange())return;closeSub();setMode('sub');toast('제출용 자료를 조회했습니다')});
$('#subPrint').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!subRange())return;closeSub();setMode('sub');try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
$('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();refresh(true)});
$('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',runQuery);
['dFrom','dTo'].forEach(id=>$('#'+id).addEventListener('change',()=>{if(mode==='sub')runQuery()}));

/* header buttons */
$('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast(CFG.loadMsg));
$('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
$('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
function setClosed(on){
  closed=on;
  $('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));
  $('#closeTxt').textContent=on?'마감취소':'마감';
  syncButtons();refresh(true);
}
$('#closeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(closed){setClosed(false);toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
  const bad=['s','p'].some(k=>['xb','xr'].some(id=>DATA[k][id].some(r=>!r.name.trim()||!r.reg.trim())));
  if(bad){toast('거래처명과 등록번호가 비어 있는 전자 외 행이 있습니다');return}
  setClosed(true);toast('마감되었습니다');
});

/* 홈택스 자료조회 = 홈택스 전자(세금)계산서 목록조회와 대사 */
$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',runRecon);

/* theme */
function applyTheme(t){document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',t)}
let theme='dark';try{theme=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
applyTheme(theme);
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{theme=theme==='dark'?'light':'dark';applyTheme(theme);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',theme)}catch(e){}});

applyPeriod();
syncButtons();
refresh(true);
runRecon(true);

</script>


======================================================================

<script>
const CFG={"doc": "계산서", "tax": false, "startK": "p", "sNames": ["(매출 없음)"], "pNames": ["엔에이치농협캐피탈 주식회사", "에이스하이엔드타워 별내지식산업센터"], "sH1": {"total": 0, "R": 0}, "pH1": {"total": 8829412, "R": 9}, "sH2": {"total": 0, "R": 0}, "pH2": {"total": 7100000, "R": 7}, "loadMsg": "홈택스 수집 계산서를 불러왔습니다"};
const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const fmt=n=>(n<0?'-':'')+Math.abs(Math.trunc(n)).toLocaleString('en-US');
const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
const dot=ym=>ym.replace('-','.');

/* ---------- sample data: one record = one invoice, dated by month ---------- */
function parts(total,n,seed){
  if(n<=0)return [];
  const w=[];let ws=0;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=1+((i*7+seed)%5)/8;w.push(x);ws+=x}
  const out=[];let acc=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n-1;i++){const v=Math.round(total*w[i]/ws/1000)*1000;out.push(v);acc+=v}
  out.push(total-acc);return out;
}
const reg=i=>String(100+(i*37)%800)+'-'+String(10+(i*13)%80)+'-'+String(10000+(i*7919)%89999);
const REPS=['김민수','이서연','박지훈','최유진','정하늘','강도윤','조은채','윤재호','임수아','한지민','오세훈','서가은','권태양','문해린'];
const H1=['2026-01','2026-02','2026-03','2026-04','2026-05','2026-06'];
const H2=['2025-07','2025-08','2025-09','2025-10','2025-11','2025-12'];
const PART={};
const EREC={s:[],p:[]};
['s','p'].forEach((k,ki)=>{
  const names=CFG[k+'Names'];
  PART[k]=names.map((n,i)=>({name:n,reg:reg(i*3+ki*3+2),rep:REPS[(i+ki*3)%REPS.length]}));
  [['H1',H1],['H2',H2]].forEach(([key,months],hi)=>{
    const d=CFG[k+key];if(!d||!d.R)return;
    parts(d.total,d.R,ki*3+hi+2).forEach((v,r)=>{
      EREC[k].push({p:r%names.length,ym:months[(r*5+(r>>1))%months.length],cnt:1,sup:v,tax:CFG.tax?Math.floor(v/10):0});
    });
  });
});
const DATA={s:{xb:[],xr:[]},p:{xb:[],xr:[]}};   /* 전자 외: entered by hand */
let cur={k:CFG.startK,t:'e',id:'b'};
let mode='file',closed=false;
let RANGE={from:'2026-01',to:'2026-06'};
const inR=ym=>ym>=RANGE.from&&ym<=RANGE.to;
const KEY=c=>c.t+c.id;

function view(k,t,id){
  if(t==='e'){
    if(id==='r')return [];
    const m=new Map();
    EREC[k].filter(r=>inR(r.ym)).forEach(r=>{
      const o=m.get(r.p)||{name:PART[k][r.p].name,reg:PART[k][r.p].reg,rep:PART[k][r.p].rep,liq:'',unit:'부',cnt:0,sup:0,tax:0};
      o.cnt+=r.cnt;o.sup+=r.sup;o.tax+=r.tax;m.set(r.p,o);
    });
    return Array.from(m.entries()).sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(e=>e[1]);
  }
  const l=DATA[k][t+id];
  return mode==='sub'?l.filter(r=>inR(r.ym)):l;
}
const sum=l=>l.reduce((o,r)=>({c:o.c+1,n:o.n+r.cnt,s:o.s+r.sup,t:o.t+r.tax}),{c:0,n:0,s:0,t:0});
const add=(a,b)=>({c:a.c+b.c,n:a.n+b.n,s:a.s+b.s,t:a.t+b.t});
const sm=(k,t,id)=>sum(view(k,t,id));
const smT=(k,t)=>add(sm(k,t,'b'),sm(k,t,'r'));

/* ---------- summary tables ---------- */
const NC=CFG.tax?6:5;
function vcells(v){return '<td class="r">'+fmt(v.c)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v.n)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v.s)+'</td>'+(CFG.tax?'<td class="r">'+fmt(v.t)+'</td>':'')}
function srow(k,t,id,label,first,T){
  const sel=cur.k===k&&cur.t===t&&cur.id===id;
  return '<tr data-k="'+k+'" data-t="'+t+'" data-id="'+id+'" class="pick-row'+(sel?' sel':'')+'" tabindex="0">'+(first?'<th class="g1" rowspan="3" scope="rowgroup">'+T+'</th>':'')+'<td class="ct">'+label+'</td>'+vcells(sm(k,t,id))+'</tr>';
}
const ssub=(k,t)=>'<tr class="sub"><td class="ct">소계</td>'+vcells(smT(k,t))+'</tr>';
function stable(k){
  const nm=k==='s'?'매출':'매입';
  const tot=add(smT(k,'e'),smT(k,'x'));
  return '<div class="sec '+(k==='s'?'sale':'buy')+'"><h3>'+nm+CFG.doc+'</h3></div>'
   +'<div class="swrap"><table class="stbl"><thead><tr><th>유형</th><th>구분</th><th>'+nm+'처</th><th>매수</th><th>공급가액</th>'+(CFG.tax?'<th>부가세</th>':'')+'</tr></thead><tbody>'
   +srow(k,'e','b','사업자',1,'전자')+srow(k,'e','r','주민번호',0)+ssub(k,'e')
   +srow(k,'x','b','사업자',1,'전자 외')+srow(k,'x','r','주민번호',0)+ssub(k,'x')
   +'<tr class="total"><td colspan="2" class="ct">합계</td>'+vcells(tot)+'</tr>'
   +'</tbody></table></div>';
}
let RECON=null;
const nowStr=()=>{const d=new Date(),p=n=>String(n).padStart(2,'0');return d.getFullYear()+'.'+p(d.getMonth()+1)+'.'+p(d.getDate())+' '+p(d.getHours())+':'+p(d.getMinutes())};
function reconRows(){
  const out=[];
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{
    const e=smT(k,'e'),h=RECON.ht[k];
    const items=[['총 매수',e.n,h.n],['총 합계금액',e.s+e.t,h.s+h.t],['총 공급가액',e.s,h.s]];
    if(CFG.tax)items.push(['총 세액',e.t,h.t]);
    items.forEach((it,i)=>out.push({k,first:i===0,n:items.length,nm:k==='s'?'매출':'매입',lb:it[0],a:it[1],b:it[2]}));
  });
  return out;
}
function reconHtml(){
  const hd='<div class="sec recon"><h3>홈택스 대사 <span class="rsub">전자'+CFG.doc+' · 홈택스 세무대리·납세관리 목록조회 기준</span></h3></div>';
  if(!RECON)return hd+'<p class="rc-empty">[홈택스 자료조회]를 누르면 홈택스 전자'+CFG.doc+' 목록조회 합계와 프로그램에 수집된 전자분 합계를 대사합니다.</p>';
  const rows=reconRows(),bad=rows.filter(r=>r.a!==r.b).length;
  const stale=RECON.from!==RANGE.from||RECON.to!==RANGE.to;
  let h=hd+'<div class="rc-meta"><span>대사일자 <b>'+RECON.at+'</b></span><span>대사기간 <b>'+dot(RECON.from)+' ~ '+dot(RECON.to)+'</b></span><span>대사결과 <b class="'+(bad?'rc-bad':'rc-ok')+'">'+(bad?'불일치 '+bad+'건':'전체 일치')+'</b></span>'+(stale?'<span class="rc-bad">조회기간이 달라졌습니다. 다시 대사하세요.</span>':'')+'</div>'
   +'<div class="swrap"><table class="stbl rtbl"><thead><tr><th>구분</th><th>항목</th><th>프로그램 (전자)</th><th>홈택스</th><th>차이</th><th>결과</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const d=r.a-r.b,ok=d===0;
    h+='<tr>'+(r.first?'<th class="g1" rowspan="'+r.n+'" scope="rowgroup">'+r.nm+'</th>':'')+'<td class="ct">'+r.lb+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.b)+'</td><td class="r'+(ok?'':' rc-bad')+'">'+fmt(d)+'</td><td class="ct"><span class="rc-b '+(ok?'rc-ok':'rc-bad')+'">'+(ok?'일치':'불일치')+'</span></td></tr>';
  });
  h+='</tbody></table></div>';
  if(bad)h+='<p class="rc-note">※ 홈택스 목록조회에는 있으나 프로그램에 수집되지 않은 전자'+CFG.doc+'가 있습니다. [불러오기]로 다시 수집한 뒤 [홈택스 자료조회]로 재대사하세요.</p>';
  return h;
}
function renderSummary(){$('#sumBody').innerHTML=stable('s')+stable('p')+reconHtml()}
/* 홈택스 목록조회 합계(견본): 프로그램 전자분과 같게 두되 CFG.mis 가 있으면 그만큼 다르게 */
function runRecon(quiet){
  const ht={};
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{const e=smT(k,'e'),m=(CFG.mis&&CFG.mis[k])||null;ht[k]={n:e.n+(m?m.n:0),s:e.s+(m?m.s:0),t:e.t+(CFG.tax&&m?Math.floor(m.s/10):0)}});
  RECON={at:nowStr(),from:RANGE.from,to:RANGE.to,ht};
  refresh(false);
  if(quiet)return;
  const bad=reconRows().filter(r=>r.a!==r.b).length;
  toast(bad?'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 불일치 '+bad+'건':'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 전체 일치',2800);
}

/* ---------- detail list ---------- */
function cols(t){
  const c=[{f:'name',l:'거래처명',k:'txt'},{f:'reg',l:cur.id==='b'?'등록번호':'주민등록번호',k:'txt'},{f:'cnt',l:'매수',k:'num'},{f:'sup',l:'공급가액',k:'num'}];
  if(CFG.tax)c.push({f:'tax',l:'부가세',k:'num'});
  c.push({f:'rep',l:'대표자명',k:'txt'});
  if(CFG.tax){c.push({f:'liq',l:'주류업종',k:'txt'});c.push({f:'unit',l:'단위신고',k:'sel'})}
  if(t==='x')c.push({f:'ym',l:'작성월',k:'month'});
  return c;
}
const titleOf=c=>'('+(c.k==='p'?'매입':'매출')+') '+(c.t==='e'?'전자':'전자 외')+' '+CFG.doc+' · '+(c.id==='b'?'사업자':'주민번호');
function detailFoot(){
  const cs=cols(cur.t),v=sm(cur.k,cur.t,cur.id);
  const total=cs.length+1+(cur.t==='x'?1:0);
  const nn=CFG.tax?3:2;
  const rest=total-3-nn;
  const num=x=>{let h='<td class="r">'+fmt(x.n)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(x.s)+'</td>';if(CFG.tax)h+='<td class="r">'+fmt(x.t)+'</td>';return h};
  const e=smT(cur.k,'e');
  $('#dFoot').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="3" class="ct">합계 ('+fmt(v.c)+'건)</td>'+num(v)+'<td colspan="'+rest+'"></td></tr>'
   +'<tr class="f2"><td colspan="3" class="ct">전자신고 마감합계</td>'+num(e)+'<td colspan="'+rest+'" class="hint">'+dot(RANGE.from)+' ~ '+dot(RANGE.to)+'</td></tr>';
}
function renderDetail(){
  const list=view(cur.k,cur.t,cur.id),edit=cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'&&!closed;
  const cs=cols(cur.t);
  $('#dTitle').textContent=titleOf(cur);
  $('#addRow').hidden=!(cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file');
  $('#addRow').disabled=closed;
  const ncol=cs.length+1+(cur.t==='x'?1:0);
  const th='<tr><th style="width:44px">No</th>'+cs.map(c=>'<th>'+c.l+'</th>').join('')+(cur.t==='x'?'<th style="width:40px"></th>':'')+'</tr>';
  let body;
  if(!list.length){
    body='<tr><td colspan="'+ncol+'"><div class="empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="30" height="30" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h8l4 4v14H6zM14 3v4h4M9 13h6M9 17h4"/></svg><span>'+(cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'?'입력된 내용이 없습니다. [+ 행 추가]로 입력하세요.':'데이터가 없습니다.')+'</span></div></td></tr>';
  }else body=list.map((r,i)=>{
    let h='<tr data-i="'+i+'"><td class="ct n">'+(i+1)+'</td>';
    cs.forEach(c=>{
      const val=r[c.f];
      if(!edit){
        if(c.k==='num')h+='<td class="r">'+fmt(val)+'</td>';
        else if(c.f==='reg')h+='<td class="ct num">'+esc(val)+'</td>';
        else if(c.f==='unit'||c.f==='ym')h+='<td class="ct">'+esc(c.f==='ym'?dot(val):val)+'</td>';
        else h+='<td>'+esc(val)+'</td>';
      }else if(c.k==='num')h+='<td><input class="cell in" data-f="'+c.f+'" inputmode="numeric" value="'+fmt(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
      else if(c.k==='sel')h+='<td><select class="txt" data-f="unit" aria-label="'+c.l+'"><option'+(val==='여'?' selected':'')+'>여</option><option'+(val==='부'?' selected':'')+'>부</option></select></td>';
      else if(c.k==='month')h+='<td><input class="txt" type="month" data-f="ym" value="'+esc(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
      else h+='<td><input class="txt'+(c.f==='reg'?' num':'')+'" data-f="'+c.f+'" value="'+esc(val)+'" aria-label="'+c.l+'"></td>';
    });
    if(cur.t==='x')h+=edit?'<td class="ct"><button type="button" class="eb" data-del="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제" title="행 삭제"><svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="12" height="12" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3.5 3.5l9 9M12.5 3.5l-9 9"/></svg></button></td>':'<td></td>';
    return h+'</tr>';
  }).join('')+'<tr class="fill"><td colspan="'+ncol+'"></td></tr>';
  $('#dWrap').innerHTML='<table class="dtbl"><thead>'+th+'</thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot id="dFoot"></tfoot></table>';
  detailFoot();
}

/* ---------- side totals ---------- */
function renderAside(){
  ['s','p'].forEach(k=>{
    const e=smT(k,'e'),x=smT(k,'x');
    $(k==='s'?'#asS':'#asP').innerHTML=
      '<div class="sumline"><span>전자</span><span class="num">'+fmt(e.s)+'</span></div>'
     +'<div class="sumline"><span>전자 외</span><span class="num">'+fmt(x.s)+'</span></div>'
     +'<div class="sumline"><span><b>합계</b></span><span class="num"><b>'+fmt(e.s+x.s)+'</b></span></div>';
  });
}
function refresh(full){renderSummary();renderAside();if(full)renderDetail();else detailFoot()}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
function toast(msg,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=msg;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function pickRow(tr){cur={k:tr.dataset.k,t:tr.dataset.t,id:tr.dataset.id};refresh(true)}
const editable=()=>cur.t==='x'&&mode==='file'&&!closed;
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const tr=e.target.closest('tr.pick-row');if(tr){pickRow(tr);return}
  const d=e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if(d&&editable()){DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)].splice(+d.dataset.del,1);refresh(true)}
});
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  const tr=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('tr.pick-row');
  if(tr&&(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' ')){e.preventDefault();pickRow(tr)}
  if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true);
  if(e.key==='Enter'&&e.target.matches('#dWrap input')){
    const list=$$('#dWrap input').filter(x=>x.offsetParent!==null),i=list.indexOf(e.target);
    if(i>-1&&list[i+1]){e.preventDefault();list[i+1].focus()}
  }
});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{
  const el=e.target;if(!el.matches('#dWrap [data-f]')||!editable())return;
  const tr=el.closest('tr'),r=DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i],f=el.dataset.f;
  if(['cnt','sup','tax'].includes(f)){
    el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');
    r[f]=parse(el.value);
    if(CFG.tax){
      if(f==='sup'&&r.auto!==false){r.tax=Math.floor(r.sup/10);tr.querySelector('[data-f="tax"]').value=fmt(r.tax)}
      if(f==='tax')r.auto=false;
    }
    refresh(false);
  }else r[f]=el.value;
});
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('#dWrap [data-f]')&&editable()){const tr=el.closest('tr');DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i][el.dataset.f]=el.value}
});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches('#dWrap input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{
  const el=e.target;
  if(el.matches('#dWrap input.cell')&&editable()){const tr=el.closest('tr');el.value=fmt(DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)][+tr.dataset.i][el.dataset.f])}
});
$('#addRow').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(!editable())return;
  DATA[cur.k][KEY(cur)].push({name:'',reg:'',cnt:0,sup:0,tax:0,rep:'',liq:'',unit:'부',ym:RANGE.to});
  refresh(true);
  const rows=$$('#dWrap tbody tr');const last=rows[rows.length-1];
  if(last){last.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'});const f=last.querySelector('input');if(f)f.focus()}
});

/* ---------- period / lookup mode ---------- */
function applyPeriod(){
  const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];
  RANGE={from:o.dataset.from.replace('.','-'),to:o.dataset.to.replace('.','-')};
  $('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;
}
function syncButtons(){
  const sub=mode==='sub',lock=sub||closed;
  ['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(id=>{$('#'+id).disabled=lock});
  $('#closeBtn').disabled=sub;
  $('#addRow').disabled=lock;
  $('#rptDate').disabled=lock;
  $('#modeChip').textContent=sub?'제출용 조회':(closed?'마감':'작성 중');
  $('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';
}
function setMode(m){
  mode=m;const sub=m==='sub';
  $('#subBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(sub));
  $('#periodSel').disabled=sub;
  const f=$('#dFrom'),t=$('#dTo');
  f.type=sub?'month':'text';t.type=sub?'month':'text';
  f.readOnly=!sub;t.readOnly=!sub;
  f.style.width=t.style.width=sub?'140px':'80px';
  if(sub){f.value=RANGE.from;t.value=RANGE.to}else applyPeriod();
  $('#dueBox').hidden=sub;$('#subHint').hidden=!sub;
  syncButtons();refresh(true);
}
function runQuery(){
  if(mode==='sub'){
    const a=$('#dFrom').value,b=$('#dTo').value;
    if(!a||!b){toast('조회 기간을 선택하세요');return}
    if(a>b){toast('시작월이 종료월보다 늦습니다');return}
    RANGE={from:a,to:b};
  }
  refresh(true);toast('조회했습니다');
}
const sm_=$('#subModal');
function openSub(){$('#sbFrom').value=RANGE.from;$('#sbTo').value=RANGE.to;sm_.hidden=false;$('#sbFrom').focus()}
function closeSub(){sm_.hidden=true}
$('#subBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(mode==='sub')setMode('file');else openSub()});
$('#subX').addEventListener('click',closeSub);$('#subCancel').addEventListener('click',closeSub);
sm_.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===sm_)closeSub()});
function subRange(){
  const a=$('#sbFrom').value,b=$('#sbTo').value;
  if(!a||!b){toast('조회 기간을 선택하세요');return false}
  if(a>b){toast('시작월이 종료월보다 늦습니다');return false}
  RANGE={from:a,to:b};return true;
}
$('#subGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!subRange())return;closeSub();setMode('sub');toast('제출용 자료를 조회했습니다')});
$('#subPrint').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!subRange())return;closeSub();setMode('sub');try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
$('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();refresh(true)});
$('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',runQuery);
['dFrom','dTo'].forEach(id=>$('#'+id).addEventListener('change',()=>{if(mode==='sub')runQuery()}));

/* header buttons */
$('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast(CFG.loadMsg));
$('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
$('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
function setClosed(on){
  closed=on;
  $('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(on));
  $('#closeTxt').textContent=on?'마감취소':'마감';
  syncButtons();refresh(true);
}
$('#closeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{
  if(closed){setClosed(false);toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
  const bad=['s','p'].some(k=>['xb','xr'].some(id=>DATA[k][id].some(r=>!r.name.trim()||!r.reg.trim())));
  if(bad){toast('거래처명과 등록번호가 비어 있는 전자 외 행이 있습니다');return}
  setClosed(true);toast('마감되었습니다');
});

/* 홈택스 자료조회 = 홈택스 전자(세금)계산서 목록조회와 대사 */
$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',runRecon);

/* theme */
function applyTheme(t){document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',t)}
let theme='dark';try{theme=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
applyTheme(theme);
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{theme=theme==='dark'?'light':'dark';applyTheme(theme);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',theme)}catch(e){}});

applyPeriod();
syncButtons();
refresh(true);
runRecon(true);

</script>

const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));

  applyPeriod();syncLock();redraw();
}

function gap(){
  const v=vis(),T=add(v);
  const row=(l,x,cls)=>'<tr class="'+(cls||'')+'"><td class="ct" style="text-align:left;padding-left:10px">'+l+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(x.c)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(x.s)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(x.t)+'</td></tr>';

   +'<td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" type="date" data-f="d" value="'+r.d+'">':r.d)+'</td>'

   +'<td>'+inp('n',r.n,'txt')+'</td><td class="ct">'+inp('r',r.r,'txt num')+'</td><td>'+inp('m',r.m,'txt')+'</td><td class="ct">'+inp('c',r.c,'txt num')+'</td><td class="ct">'+inp('a',r.a,'txt')+'</td>'
   +'<td class="ct">'+(ed?'<button type="button" class="eb" data-del="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제">✕</button>':'')+'</td></tr>'}).join('');

  $('#addRow').disabled=closed;
}

  if(['cnt','s','t'].includes(f)){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');r[f]=parse(el.value);if(f==='s'&&r.auto!==false){r.t=Math.floor(r.s/10);el.closest('tr').querySelector('[data-f=t]').value=fmt(r.t)}if(f==='t')r.auto=false;gap();foot()}

function bsStat(r){const k=(r||'').replace(/\D/g,'');if(k.length!==10)return null;const f=BSMAP[r];if(f)return f;const d=+k[9];return d===0?['폐업자','-','2026-03-31']:d===1?['휴업자','일반과세자','']:d===9?['계속사업자','간이과세자','']:['계속사업자','일반과세자','']}
let BS_AT='';
function bsDraw(){
  const m=new Map();L.forEach(r=>{if(r.r&&!m.has(r.r))m.set(r.r,r.n)});
  const rows=[...m.entries()];
  $('#bsBody').innerHTML=rows.length?rows.map(e=>{const st=BS_AT?bsStat(e[0]):null;return '<tr><td>'+esc(e[1])+'</td><td class="ct">'+esc(e[0])+'</td><td class="ct'+(st&&st[0]!=='계속사업자'?' rc-bad':'')+'">'+(st?st[0]:'-')+'</td><td class="ct">'+(st?st[1]:'-')+'</td><td class="ct">'+(st&&st[2]?st[2]:'-')+'</td></tr>'}).join(''):'<tr><td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);padding:14px">조회할 거래처가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
  $('#bsAt').textContent=BS_AT?'조회일시 '+BS_AT:'조회 전';
}

  });

function draw(){
  const T={c:0,h:0,e:0};R.forEach(r=>C.forEach(f=>T[f]+=r[f]));

function rc(){
  const box=$('#rcBox');if(!box)return;
  const hd='<div class="sec recon"><h3>홈택스 대사 <span class="rsub">신용카드·판매(결제)대행 매출자료 조회 기준</span></h3></div>';

  const rows=rcRows()[1],bad=rows.filter(r=>r[2]!==null&&r[1]!==r[2]).length;

   +'<div class="swrap"><table class="stbl rtbl"><thead><tr><th rowspan="2">항목</th><th rowspan="2">프로그램</th><th colspan="2">홈택스</th><th rowspan="2">차이</th><th rowspan="2">결과</th></tr><tr><th>신용카드·제로페이 매출액계</th><th>판매(결제)대행 신용카드</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
   +rows.map(r=>{const na=r[2]===null,d=na?0:r[1]-r[2],ok=d===0;
     const ht=r[3]==='two'?'<td class="r">'+fmt(RC.ht.a)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(RC.ht.b)+'</td>':r[3]==='sum'?'<td class="r" colspan="2">'+fmt(r[2])+'</td>':'<td class="ct" colspan="2">-</td>';
     return '<tr><td class="ct">'+r[0]+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r[1])+'</td>'+ht+'<td class="r'+(ok?'':' rc-bad')+'">'+(na?'-':fmt(d))+'</td><td class="ct">'+(na?'-':'<span class="rc-b '+(ok?'rc-ok':'rc-bad')+'">'+(ok?'일치':'불일치')+'</span>')+'</td></tr>'}).join('')+'</tbody></table></div>'
   +(bad?'<p class="rc-note">※ 홈택스 매출자료와 프로그램 입력 금액이 다릅니다. 입력 내용을 확인·수정한 뒤 [홈택스 자료조회]로 재대사하세요.</p>':'');
}
function runRc(q){RC={at:nowS2(),from:$('#dFrom').value,to:$('#dTo').value,ht:HT};rc();if(!q){const bad=rcRows()[1].filter(r=>r[2]!==null&&r[1]!==r[2]).length;toast(bad?'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 불일치 '+bad+'건':'홈택스와 대사했습니다 · 전체 일치',2800)}}
$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>runRc(false));
bindCommon(draw,null);
runRc(true);

</script>

function drawSup(){$('#sup').innerHTML=SUPS.map((s,i)=>'<button type="button" class="sli" data-sel="'+i+'"'+(i===SEL?' aria-current="true"':'')+'><span>'+esc(s.n||'(신규)')+'</span><span class="num">'+esc(s.r)+'</span></button>').join('')}
function drawList(){
  const ed=!closed,s=SUPS[SEL];
  $('#dT').textContent=s?s.n+' · 매입 내역':'';
  const rows=ROWS.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(o=>o.r.s===SEL&&inR(o.r.d));
  const sup=ed&&s?'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px"><input class="txt" id="sN" placeholder="공급자(상호)" value="'+esc(s.n)+'"><input class="txt num" id="sR" placeholder="사업자/주민등록번호" value="'+esc(s.r)+'"></div>':'';
  let b=rows.map(o=>{const r=o.r,i=o.i;return '<tr data-i="'+i+'"><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" type="date" data-f="d" value="'+r.d+'">':r.d)+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<select class="txt" data-f="k">'+KD.map((k,j)=>'<option value="'+j+'"'+(j===r.k?' selected':'')+'>'+k+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':KD[r.k])+'</td>'
   +'<td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-f="it" value="'+esc(r.it)+'">':esc(r.it))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-f="q" value="'+esc(r.q)+'" style="width:80px">':esc(r.q))+'</td>'
   +'<td'+(ed?'':' class="r"')+'>'+(ed?'<input class="cell in" data-f="a" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.a)+'">':f(r.a))+'</td><td class="ct">'+(ed&&(RS().length>1||!RS().includes(rrate(r)))?'<select class="txt" data-f="rate">'+(RS().includes(rrate(r))?RS():RS().concat([rrate(r)])).map(x=>'<option'+(x===rrate(r)?' selected':'')+'>'+x+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':rrate(r))+'</td><td class="r rtx">'+f(tx(r))+'</td>'
   +'<td'+(ed?'':' class="r"')+'>'+(ed?'<input class="cell in" data-f="c" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.c)+'" style="width:60px">':f(r.c))+'</td><td class="ct">'+(ed?'<button type="button" class="eb" data-del="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제">✕</button>':'')+'</td></tr>'}).join('');
  if(!rows.length)b='<tr><td colspan="9"><div class="empty"><span>매입 내역이 없습니다. [+ 행 추가]로 입력하세요.</span></div></td></tr>';
  $('#eul').innerHTML=sup+'<table class="dtbl"><thead><tr><th>취득일자</th><th>구분</th><th>물품명</th><th>수량</th><th>매입가액</th><th>공제율</th><th>의제매입세액</th><th>건수</th><th style="width:34px"></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+b+'</tbody><tfoot><tr id="dFoot"></tr></tfoot></table>';
  $('#addRow').disabled=closed||!s;$('#addSup').disabled=closed;$('#bt').disabled=closed;$('#ind').disabled=closed;$('#rptDate').disabled=closed;
}

<script>

const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const fmt=n=>(n<0?'-':'')+Math.abs(Math.trunc(n)).toLocaleString('en-US');
const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
let closed=false;
function toast(m,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=m;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function applyPeriod(){const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];$('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;}
function syncLock(){['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(i=>$('#'+i).disabled=closed);$('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';$('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';$$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed)}
function bindCommon(redraw,validate){
  $('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();redraw()});
  $('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{redraw();toast('조회했습니다')});
  $('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('환경등록 연동 자료를 불러왔습니다'));
  $('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
  $('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
  const cb=$('#closeBtn');if(cb)cb.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(closed){closed=false;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
    const m=validate&&validate();if(m){toast(m);return}
    closed=true;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감취소';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감되었습니다');
  });
  const ht=$('#htModal');if(ht&&$('#htBtn')){$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=false;$('#htClose').focus()});
  $('#htClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true});ht.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ht)ht.hidden=true});
  $('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true;toast('선택한 자료를 조회합니다')})}
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true)});
  document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches('input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
  let th='dark';try{th=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);
  $('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{th=th==='dark'?'light':'dark';document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',th)}catch(e){}});
  applyPeriod();syncLock();redraw();
}

function defRate(){const i=S.ind,b=S.bt,x=baseTot();

 if(i==='c')return b==='p'?'6/106':'2/102';
 if(i==='d')return'4/104';return'2/102'}
function defLim(x,ann){if(S.bt==='c')return 50;
 if(ann)return x<=400000000?65:55;
 if(isFood())return x<=100000000?75:(x<=200000000?70:60);
 return x<=200000000?65:55}
