function drawSup(){$('#sup').innerHTML=SUPS.map((s,i)=>'<button type="button" class="sli" data-sel="'+i+'"'+(i===SEL?' aria-current="true"':'')+'><span>'+esc(s.n||'(신규)')+'</span><span class="num">'+esc(s.r)+'</span></button>').join('')}
function drawList(){
  const ed=!closed,s=SUPS[SEL];
  $('#dT').textContent=s?s.n+' · 매입 내역':'';
  const rows=ROWS.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(o=>o.r.s===SEL&&inR(o.r.d));
  const sup=ed&&s?'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px"><input class="txt" id="sN" placeholder="공급자(상호)" value="'+esc(s.n)+'"><input class="txt num" id="sR" placeholder="사업자/주민등록번호" value="'+esc(s.r)+'"></div>':'';
  let b=rows.map(o=>{const r=o.r,i=o.i;return '<tr data-i="'+i+'"><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" type="date" data-f="d" value="'+r.d+'">':r.d)+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<select class="txt" data-f="k">'+KD.map((k,j)=>'<option value="'+j+'"'+(j===r.k?' selected':'')+'>'+k+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':KD[r.k])+'</td>'
   +'<td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-f="it" value="'+esc(r.it)+'">':esc(r.it))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-f="q" value="'+esc(r.q)+'" style="width:80px">':esc(r.q))+'</td>'
   +'<td'+(ed?'':' class="r"')+'>'+(ed?'<input class="cell in" data-f="a" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.a)+'">':f(r.a))+'</td><td class="ct">'+(ed&&(RS().length>1||!RS().includes(rrate(r)))?'<select class="txt" data-f="rate">'+(RS().includes(rrate(r))?RS():RS().concat([rrate(r)])).map(x=>'<option'+(x===rrate(r)?' selected':'')+'>'+x+'</option>').join('')+'</select>':rrate(r))+'</td><td class="r rtx">'+f(tx(r))+'</td>'
   +'<td'+(ed?'':' class="r"')+'>'+(ed?'<input class="cell in" data-f="c" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.c)+'" style="width:60px">':f(r.c))+'</td><td class="ct">'+(ed?'<button type="button" class="eb" data-del="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제">✕</button>':'')+'</td></tr>'}).join('');
  if(!rows.length)b='<tr><td colspan="9"><div class="empty"><span>매입 내역이 없습니다. [+ 행 추가]로 입력하세요.</span></div></td></tr>';
  $('#eul').innerHTML=sup+'<table class="dtbl"><thead><tr><th>취득일자</th><th>구분</th><th>물품명</th><th>수량</th><th>매입가액</th><th>공제율</th><th>의제매입세액</th><th>건수</th><th style="width:34px"></th></tr></thead><tbody>'+b+'</tbody><tfoot><tr id="dFoot"></tr></tfoot></table>';
  $('#addRow').disabled=closed||!s;$('#addSup').disabled=closed;$('#bt').disabled=closed;$('#ind').disabled=closed;$('#rptDate').disabled=closed;
}
function redraw(){$('#bt').value=S.bt;$('#ind').value=S.ind;drawSup();drawList();drawCalc();updNums()}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const sl=e.target.closest('[data-sel]');if(sl){SEL=+sl.dataset.sel;drawSup();drawList();updNums();return}
  const d=e.target.closest('[data-del]');if(d&&!closed){ROWS.splice(+d.dataset.del,1);drawList();updNums()}});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed)return;
  if(el.id==='sN'){SUPS[SEL].n=el.value;$('#dT').textContent=el.value+' · 매입 내역';return}
  if(el.id==='sR'){SUPS[SEL].r=el.value;return}
  if(el.dataset.s){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');S[el.dataset.s]=parse(el.value);updNums();return}
  if(el.dataset.m){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');S.m[el.dataset.m]=parse(el.value);updNums();return}
  if(el.matches('#eul [data-f]')){const r=ROWS[+el.closest('tr').dataset.i],fl=el.dataset.f;
    if(fl==='a'||fl==='c'){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');r[fl]=parse(el.value);updNums()}else if(fl!=='k'&&fl!=='d')r[fl]=el.value}});
document.addEventListener('change',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed)return;
  if(el.id==='bt'||el.id==='ind'){S[el.id]=el.value;S.limOv=null;S.ri=null;S.m.lim=null;S.m.rate=null;remapRates();drawCalc();drawList();updNums();return}
  if(el.dataset.sl){S.limOv=+el.value;updNums();return}
  if(el.dataset.sr){S.ri=+el.value;remapRates();updNums();drawList();return}
  if(el.dataset.ml){S.m.lim=+el.value;updNums();return}
  if(el.dataset.mr){S.m.rate=el.value;updNums();return}

    if(fl==='rate'){r.rate=el.value;updNums()}else if(fl==='k'){r.k=+el.value;updNums()}else if(fl==='d'){r.d=el.value;drawList();updNums()}}});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed)return;
  if(el.dataset.s)el.value=f(S[el.dataset.s]);else if(el.dataset.m)el.value=f(S.m[el.dataset.m]);
  else if(el.matches('#eul input.cell'))el.value=f(ROWS[+el.closest('tr').dataset.i][el.dataset.f])});

function drawNs(){
  const sups=W.sups,empty='<div class="empty"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="34" height="34" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.4" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 3h8l4 4v14H6zM14 3v4h4M9 13h6M9 17h4"/></svg><span>데이터가 없습니다.</span></div>';
  $('#nsSup').innerHTML=sups.length?sups.map((s,i)=>'<button type="button" class="sli" data-nsel="'+i+'"'+(i===WSEL?' aria-current="true"':'')+'><span>'+esc(s.n||'(신규)')+'</span><span class="num">'+esc(s.r)+'</span></button>').join(''):empty;
  const rows=W.rows.map((r,i)=>({r,i})).filter(o=>o.r.s===WSEL),cur=sups[WSEL];
  const head='<table class="dtbl"><thead><tr><th>취득일자</th><th>구분</th><th>물품명</th><th>수량</th><th>매입가액</th><th>공제율</th><th>의제매입세액</th><th>건수</th><th style="width:34px"></th></tr></thead>';
  const rs=RS();
  const body=rows.map(o=>{const r=o.r,i=o.i;return '<tr data-ni="'+i+'"><td><input class="txt" type="date" data-nf="d" value="'+r.d+'"></td><td><select class="txt" data-nf="k">'+KD.map((k,j)=>'<option value="'+j+'"'+(j===r.k?' selected':'')+'>'+k+'</option>').join('')+'</select></td><td><input class="txt" data-nf="it" value="'+esc(r.it)+'"></td><td><input class="txt" data-nf="q" value="'+esc(r.q)+'" style="width:80px"></td><td><input class="cell in" data-nf="a" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.a)+'"></td><td class="ct"><select class="txt" data-nf="rate">'+(rs.includes(rrate(r))?rs:rs.concat([rrate(r)])).map(x=>'<option'+(x===rrate(r)?' selected':'')+'>'+x+'</option>').join('')+'</select></td><td class="r ntx">'+f(tx(r))+'</td><td><input class="cell in" data-nf="c" inputmode="numeric" value="'+f(r.c)+'" style="width:60px"></td><td class="ct"><button type="button" class="eb" data-ndel="'+i+'" aria-label="행 삭제">✕</button></td></tr>'}).join('');
  const sx=nsTot({rows:rows.map(o=>o.r)});
  $('#nsTbl').innerHTML=(cur?'<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px"><input class="txt" data-ns="n" placeholder="공급자(상호)" value="'+esc(cur.n)+'"><input class="txt num" data-ns="r" placeholder="사업자(주민)번호" value="'+esc(cur.r)+'"></div>':'')+(rows.length?head+'<tbody>'+body+'</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="4" class="ct">합계</td><td class="r" id="nsSa">'+f(sx.a)+'</td><td></td><td class="r" id="nsSt">'+f(sx.t)+'</td><td class="r" id="nsSc">'+f(sx.c)+'</td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table>':head+'<tbody><tr><td colspan="9">'+empty+'</td></tr></tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="4" class="ct">합계</td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td></td></tr></tfoot></table>');
  $('#nsAddRow').disabled=!cur;
  updNsTot();
}
function updNsTot(){const x=nsTot(W);$('#nsA').value=f(x.a);$('#nsT').value=f(x.t);
  const rows=W.rows.filter(r=>r.s===WSEL);const sx=nsTot({rows});const a=$('#nsSa');if(a){a.textContent=f(sx.a);$('#nsSt').textContent=f(sx.t);$('#nsSc').textContent=f(sx.c)}
  $$('#nsTbl tbody tr[data-ni]').forEach(tr=>{tr.querySelector('.ntx').textContent=f(tx(W.rows[+tr.dataset.ni]))})}

  return ''});

const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));

const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
let closed=false;
function toast(m,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=m;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function applyPeriod(){const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];$('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;}
function syncLock(){['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(i=>$('#'+i).disabled=closed);$('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';$('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';$$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed)}

  $('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();redraw()});
  $('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{redraw();toast('조회했습니다')});
  $('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
  $('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
  const cb=$('#closeBtn');if(cb)cb.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(closed){closed=false;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
    const m=validate&&validate();if(m){toast(m);return}
    closed=true;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감취소';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감되었습니다');
  });
  const ht=$('#htModal');if(ht&&$('#htBtn')){$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=false;$('#htClose').focus()});
  $('#htClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true});ht.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ht)ht.hidden=true});
  $('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true;toast('선택한 자료를 조회합니다')})}
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true)});

  applyPeriod();syncLock();redraw();
}

/* 정기예금 이자율 (시행규칙 제47조) — 과세기간 종료일 현재 */
const RATES=[['2025-03-21','9999-12-31',3.1],['2024-03-22','2025-03-20',3.5],['2023-03-20','2024-03-21',2.9],['2021-03-16','2023-03-19',1.2]];
let SEL=0,P0='2026-01-01',P1='2026-06-30';
const D=s=>{const a=s.split('-').map(Number);return Date.UTC(a[0],a[1]-1,a[2])/864e5};
const dim=(y,m)=>new Date(y,m,0).getDate();
const leap=y=>(y%4===0&&y%100!==0)||y%400===0;
const rateAt=d=>{const r=RATES.find(x=>d>=x[0]&&d<=x[1]);return r?r[2]:3.1};
function per(){const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];const f=o.dataset.from.split('.'),t=o.dataset.to.split('.');P0=f[0]+'-'+f[1]+'-01';P1=t[0]+'-'+t[1]+'-'+String(dim(+t[0],+t[1])).padStart(2,'0')}
const RATE=()=>rateAt(P1);
function calc(c,t){
  if(!c.f||!c.t)return null;
  const a=Math.max(D(c.f),D(P0)),b=Math.min(D(c.t),D(P1));if(b<a)return null;
  const days=b-a+1,Y=leap(+P1.slice(0,4))?366:365;
  let rent=0,mng=0;const y0=+P0.slice(0,4),m0=+P0.slice(5,7),y1=+P1.slice(0,4),m1=+P1.slice(5,7);
  for(let y=y0,m=m0;y<y1||(y===y1&&m<=m1);m++){if(m>12){m=1;y++;if(y>y1)break}
    const dm=dim(y,m),ms=Date.UTC(y,m-1,1)/864e5,me=ms+dm-1,o=Math.min(b,me)-Math.max(a,ms)+1;
    if(o>0){rent+=t.rent*o/dm;mng+=t.mng*o/dm}}
  const r10=Math.round(RATE()*10);
  return {a,b,days,rent:Math.round(rent),mng:Math.round(mng),int:Math.round(t.dep*r10*days/(1000*Y))};
}
const ds=n=>{const d=new Date(n*864e5);return d.getUTCFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getUTCMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getUTCDate()).padStart(2,'0')};
function tsum(t){const o={rent:0,mng:0,int:0};t.c.forEach(c=>{const k=calc(c,t);if(k){o.rent+=k.rent;o.mng+=k.mng;o.int+=k.int}});o.tax=o.rent+o.mng+o.int;return o}

  const ed=!closed;
  $('#tWrap').innerHTML='<table class="tl"><thead><tr><th>No</th><th>코드</th><th>거래처명(임차인)</th><th>동</th><th>층</th><th>호</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
   +TEN.map((t,i)=>'<tr data-i="'+i+'"'+(i===SEL?' class="sel"':'')+'><td class="n">'+(i+1)+'</td>'
    +'<td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-t="code" value="'+esc(t.code)+'" style="text-align:center">':esc(t.code))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-t="n" value="'+esc(t.n)+'">':esc(t.n))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-t="dong" value="'+esc(t.dong)+'">':esc(t.dong))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-t="fl" value="'+esc(t.fl)+'">':esc(t.fl))+'</td><td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-t="ho" value="'+esc(t.ho)+'">':esc(t.ho))+'</td>'
    +'</tr>').join('')+'<tr class="fill"><td colspan="6"></td></tr></tbody></table>';
  $('#addT').disabled=closed;
}
function drawD(){
  const t=TEN[SEL],ed=!closed;
  if(!t){$('#regBox').innerHTML='';$('#cWrap').innerHTML='';$('#kWrap').innerHTML='';$('#tTitle').textContent='등록사항';return}
  $('#tTitle').textContent=(t.n||'(신규)')+' · 등록사항';
  const f=(l,k,ph)=>'<label>'+l+'</label>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" data-r="'+k+'" value="'+esc(t[k])+'"'+(ph?' placeholder="'+ph+'"':'')+'>':'<span>'+esc(t[k])+'</span>');
  const fn=(l,k)=>'<label>'+l+'</label>'+(ed?'<input class="cell in" data-rn="'+k+'" inputmode="numeric" value="'+fmt(t[k])+'" style="text-align:right">':'<span class="num">'+fmt(t[k])+'</span>');
  $('#regBox').innerHTML=f('사업자등록번호','reg','000-00-00000')+f('주민등록번호','rrn','')+f('면적(㎡)','area','')+f('용도','use','')+fn('보증금','dep')+fn('월세','rent')+fn('관리비','mng');
  $('#addC').disabled=closed;
  $('#cWrap').innerHTML='<table class="tl"><thead><tr><th>No</th><th>계약갱신일</th><th>임대기간 시작</th><th>임대기간 종료</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
   +t.c.map((c,i)=>'<tr data-ci="'+i+'"><td class="n">'+(i+1)+'</td>'+['ren','f','t'].map(k=>'<td>'+(ed?'<input class="txt" type="date" data-c="'+k+'" value="'+c[k]+'" style="min-width:128px">':c[k])+'</td>').join('')
    +'</tr>').join('')+(t.c.length?'':'<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);padding:14px">계약 내용이 없습니다. [+ 행 추가]로 입력하세요.</td></tr>')+'</tbody></table>';
  drawK();
}
function drawK(){
  const t=TEN[SEL];if(!t)return;
  const ks=t.c.map(c=>calc(c,t)),s=tsum(t);
  $('#calcHint').textContent='적용이자율 '+RATE()+'% · '+(leap(+P1.slice(0,4))?366:365)+'일 기준';
  const days=ks.reduce((n,k)=>n+(k?k.days:0),0);
  $('#kWrap').innerHTML='<table class="tl"><thead><tr><th>과세기간</th><th>일수</th><th>월세</th><th>관리비</th><th>간주임대료</th><th>계(과세표준)</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="ct">'+P0+' ~ '+P1+'</td><td class="r">'+days+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(s.rent)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(s.mng)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(s.int)+'</td><td class="r"><b>'+fmt(s.tax)+'</b></td></tr></tbody></table>';
}
function drawTot(){
  const T=TEN.reduce((o,t)=>{const s=tsum(t);o.rent+=s.rent;o.mng+=s.mng;o.int+=s.int;o.tax+=s.tax;return o},{rent:0,mng:0,int:0,tax:0});
  $('#totWrap').innerHTML='<table class="stbl"><thead><tr><th>월세 등</th><th>간주임대료</th><th>과세표준(계)</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr class="total"><td class="r">'+fmt(T.rent+T.mng)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(T.int)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(T.tax)+'</td></tr></tbody></table>';
}
function redraw(){$('#rcBtn').disabled=closed;per();$('#rate').value=RATE();drawT();drawD();drawTot()}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const dt=e.target.closest('[data-dt]');if(dt&&!closed){TEN.splice(+dt.dataset.dt,1);SEL=Math.min(SEL,TEN.length-1);redraw();return}
  const dc=e.target.closest('[data-dc]');if(dc&&!closed){TEN[SEL].c.splice(+dc.dataset.dc,1);drawD();drawTot();return}
  const tr=e.target.closest('#tWrap tbody tr');if(tr&&+tr.dataset.i!==SEL){SEL=+tr.dataset.i;drawT();drawD()}
});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{const tr=e.target.closest&&e.target.closest('#tWrap tbody tr');if(tr&&+tr.dataset.i!==SEL){SEL=+tr.dataset.i;$$('#tWrap tbody tr').forEach(r=>r.classList.toggle('sel',+r.dataset.i===SEL));drawD()}});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed)return;
  if(el.dataset.t!==undefined){const i=+el.closest('tr').dataset.i;TEN[i][el.dataset.t]=el.value;if(i===SEL&&el.dataset.t==='n')$('#tTitle').textContent=(el.value||'(신규)')+' · 등록사항';return}
  if(el.dataset.r){TEN[SEL][el.dataset.r]=el.value;return}
  if(el.dataset.rn){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');TEN[SEL][el.dataset.rn]=parse(el.value);drawK();drawTot();drawT();return}
  if(el.dataset.c){const c=TEN[SEL].c[+el.closest('tr').dataset.ci],k=el.dataset.c;
    c[k]=el.value;drawK();drawTot()}});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const el=e.target;if(el.matches&&el.matches('input.cell[data-rn]')&&!closed)el.value=fmt(TEN[SEL][el.dataset.rn])});
$('#addT').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed)return;TEN.push({n:'',code:String(1001+TEN.length),dong:'',fl:'',ho:'',reg:'',rrn:'',area:'',use:'',dep:0,rent:0,mng:0,c:[]});SEL=TEN.length-1;redraw();const x=$('#tWrap tbody tr:last-child input');if(x)x.focus()});
$('#addC').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed||!TEN[SEL])return;TEN[SEL].c.push({ren:'',f:P0,t:P1});drawD();drawTot()});
/* popups */
const MD=id=>$('#'+id);const open_=id=>{MD(id).hidden=false};const close_=id=>{MD(id).hidden=true};
['ldModal','rtModal','qrModal'].forEach(id=>MD(id).addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===MD(id))close_(id)}));
$('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!closed)open_('ldModal')});
$('#ldCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>close_('ldModal'));
$('#ldOk').addEventListener('click',()=>{const n=$$('#ldModal [data-ld]:checked').length;if(!n){toast('불러올 신고기간을 선택하세요');return}close_('ldModal');toast('선택한 신고기간의 임대 내역을 불러왔습니다')});
$$('#ldModal [data-tab]').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>{$$('#ldModal [data-tab]').forEach(x=>x.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(x===b)));MD('ldP1').hidden=b.dataset.tab!=='1';MD('ldP2').hidden=b.dataset.tab!=='2'}));
$('#rtBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>open_('rtModal'));$('#rtOk').addEventListener('click',()=>close_('rtModal'));

/* F2 거래처코드도움 */
const CUST=[
 {c:'1001',n:'청담휘트니스',r:'219-82-69711',p:'김청담'},{c:'1002',n:'햇살약국',r:'515-31-00641',p:'이햇살'},{c:'1003',n:'맛고을식당',r:'107-81-62334',p:'박맛고'},{c:'1004',n:'스마일치과',r:'305-81-17764',p:'최스마'},
 {c:'1005',n:'석금당',r:'214-81-35210',p:'정석금'},{c:'1006',n:'세븐일레븐율량',r:'120-86-48217',p:'한세븐'},{c:'1007',n:'오구피자',r:'409-12-33865',p:'오구피'},{c:'1008',n:'우리가스',r:'729-71-54624',p:'윤우리'},{c:'1009',n:'새봄카페',r:'611-27-90318',p:'서새봄'},{c:'1010',n:'미래학원',r:'138-15-72046',p:'강미래'}];
let CDROW=-1,CDSEL=-1;
function cdDraw(){
  const q=$('#cdQ').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  const l=CUST.map((x,i)=>({x,i})).filter(o=>!q||o.x.c.includes(q)||o.x.n.toLowerCase().includes(q));
  if(!l.some(o=>o.i===CDSEL))CDSEL=l.length?l[0].i:-1;
  $('#cdBody').innerHTML=l.map(o=>'<tr data-k="'+o.i+'"'+(o.i===CDSEL?' class="sel"':'')+'><td class="ct">'+o.x.c+'</td><td>'+esc(o.x.n)+'</td><td class="ct">'+o.x.r+'</td><td>'+esc(o.x.p)+'</td></tr>').join('')||'<tr><td colspan="4" style="text-align:center;color:var(--muted);padding:14px">검색 결과가 없습니다.</td></tr>';
}
function cdOpen(row){if(closed)return;CDROW=row;const t=TEN[row];$('#cdQ').value='';CDSEL=CUST.findIndex(x=>x.c===t.code&&t.code);cdDraw();MD('cdModal').hidden=false;$('#cdQ').focus()}
function cdPick(){
  if(CDSEL<0)return;const x=CUST[CDSEL],t=TEN[CDROW];if(!t)return;
  t.code=x.c;t.n=x.n;t.reg=x.r;MD('cdModal').hidden=true;SEL=CDROW;redraw();toast('거래처를 반영했습니다')}
document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key==='F2'){const el=e.target;if(el.matches&&el.matches('#tWrap input[data-t="code"],#tWrap input[data-t="n"]')){e.preventDefault();cdOpen(+el.closest('tr').dataset.i)}}
  if(!MD('cdModal').hidden){
    if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();cdPick()}
    if(e.key==='ArrowDown'||e.key==='ArrowUp'){e.preventDefault();const ks=$$('#cdBody tr[data-k]').map(r=>+r.dataset.k),i=ks.indexOf(CDSEL);const n=ks[Math.max(0,Math.min(ks.length-1,i+(e.key==='ArrowDown'?1:-1)))];if(n!==undefined){CDSEL=n;cdDraw()}}}
});
$('#cdQ').addEventListener('input',cdDraw);
$('#cdBody').addEventListener('click',e=>{const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-k]');if(tr){CDSEL=+tr.dataset.k;cdDraw()}});
$('#cdBody').addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{const tr=e.target.closest('tr[data-k]');if(tr){CDSEL=+tr.dataset.k;cdPick()}});
$('#cdOk').addEventListener('click',cdPick);$('#cdCancel').addEventListener('click',()=>MD('cdModal').hidden=true);
MD('cdModal').addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===MD('cdModal'))MD('cdModal').hidden=true});
$('#delBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed||!TEN[SEL])return;const n=TEN[SEL].n||'(신규)';TEN.splice(SEL,1);SEL=Math.max(0,Math.min(SEL,TEN.length-1));redraw();toast(n+' 임차인을 삭제했습니다')});
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const d=e.data;if(d&&d.type==='vat-set-closed'){closed=!!d.closed;syncLock();redraw()}});
bindCommon(redraw,null);

</script>

 <section class="card"><div class="card-h"><h2>전자세금계산서 발급세액공제 대상 여부(⑥)</h2></div>

<script>

const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const fmt=n=>(n<0?'-':'')+Math.abs(Math.trunc(n)).toLocaleString('en-US');
const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
let closed=false;
function toast(m,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=m;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function applyPeriod(){const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];$('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;}
function syncLock(){['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(i=>$('#'+i).disabled=closed);$('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';$('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';$$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed)}
function bindCommon(redraw,validate){
  $('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();redraw()});
  $('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{redraw();toast('조회했습니다')});
  $('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
  $('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
  const cb=$('#closeBtn');if(cb)cb.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(closed){closed=false;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
    const m=validate&&validate();if(m){toast(m);return}
    closed=true;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감취소';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감되었습니다');
  });
  const ht=$('#htModal');if(ht&&$('#htBtn')){$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=false;$('#htClose').focus()});
  $('#htClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true});ht.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ht)ht.hidden=true});
  $('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true;toast('선택한 자료를 조회합니다')})}
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true)});
  document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches('input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
  let th='dark';try{th=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);
  $('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{th=th==='dark'?'light':'dark';document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',th)}catch(e){}});
  applyPeriod();syncLock();redraw();
}

const UNIT=200,LIMIT=1000000,CAP=300000000;
const PROG=[12,15,9,14,18,11,0,0,0,0,0,0];
const EXT=[0,0,3,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0,0];
let PREV=0;
const PREV_PL=185420000;
const dm=()=>{const f=$('#dFrom').value.split('.'),t=$('#dTo').value.split('.');return [+f[1]||1,+t[1]||12]};
function calc(){
  const ok=PREV>0&&PREV<CAP;
  const tot=PROG.map((p,i)=>p+EXT[i]);
  const amt=tot.map(t=>t*UNIT);
  const real=[0,0,0,0],qc=[0,0,0,0],qa=[0,0,0,0];let cum=0;const cumQ=[];
  for(let q=0;q<4;q++){for(let k=0;k<3;k++){qc[q]+=tot[q*3+k];qa[q]+=amt[q*3+k]}
    real[q]=ok?Math.max(0,Math.min(qa[q],LIMIT-cum)):0;cum+=real[q];cumQ[q]=cum}
  return {ok,tot,amt,real,qc,qa,cumQ};
}
function draw(){
  const c=calc(),[m1,m2]=dm();
  $('#elig').innerHTML=PREV>0?'<span class="yn '+(c.ok?'y':'n')+'">'+(c.ok?'여':'부')+'</span>':'';
  const n7=c.tot.slice(m1-1,m2).reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0),r9=n7*UNIT;
  let r12=0;for(let q=0;q<4;q++){if(q*3+3<m1)r12+=c.real[q]}
  const r13=LIMIT-r12,r10=c.ok?Math.min(r9,r13):0;
  $('#calcA').innerHTML='<thead><tr><th>⑦ 전자세금계산서<br>발급건수</th><th>⑧ 건당 공제금액</th><th>⑨ 공제가능세액<br>( ⑦ x ⑧ )</th><th>⑩ 해당 공제세액<br>( ⑨ 과 ⑬ 중 적은 금액 )</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="r">'+fmt(n7)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(UNIT)+'원</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r9)+'</td><td class="r bign">'+fmt(r10)+'</td></tr></tbody>';
  $('#calcB').innerHTML='<thead><tr><th>⑪ 연간 공제한도액</th><th>⑫ 기 공제세액</th><th>⑬ 해당 과세기간 공제한도액<br>( ⑪ - ⑫ )</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td class="r">'+fmt(LIMIT)+'원</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r12)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r13)+'</td></tr></tbody>';
  const labs=['1~3월','4~6월','7~9월','10~12월'];
  $('#amtT').innerHTML='<thead><tr><th>신고기간</th><th>건수</th><th>실공제세액</th><th>누적공제세액</th><th>공제가능액</th></tr></thead><tbody>'+labs.map((l,q)=>'<tr><td class="rl">'+l+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.qc[q])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.real[q])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.cumQ[q])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(LIMIT-c.cumQ[q])+'</td></tr>').join('')+'</tbody>';
  let h='<thead><tr><th rowspan="2" style="width:82px">기 간</th><th colspan="3">전자세금계산서 건수</th><th rowspan="2">공제대상액</th><th rowspan="2">실공제액</th></tr><tr><th>프로그램</th><th>외부</th><th>합계</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  let yp=0,ye=0,yt=0,ya=0,yr=0;
  for(let h2=0;h2<2;h2++){let hp=0,he=0,ht=0,ha=0,hr=0;
    for(let q=h2*2;q<h2*2+2;q++){let sp=0,se=0;
      for(let k=0;k<3;k++){const i=q*3+k;sp+=PROG[i];se+=EXT[i];
        h+='<tr><td class="rl">'+(i+1)+'월</td><td class="r">'+fmt(PROG[i])+'</td><td class="ed"><input class="cell num" data-edit data-x="'+i+'" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="off" value="'+fmt(EXT[i])+'" aria-label="'+(i+1)+'월 외부 건수"></td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.tot[i])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.amt[i])+'</td><td></td></tr>'}
      h+='<tr class="sub"><td>분기 계</td><td class="r">'+fmt(sp)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(se)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.qc[q])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.qa[q])+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c.real[q])+'</td></tr>';
      hp+=sp;he+=se;ht+=c.qc[q];ha+=c.qa[q];hr+=c.real[q]}
    h+='<tr class="sub"><td>'+(h2+1)+'기 소계</td><td class="r">'+fmt(hp)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(he)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(ht)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(ha)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(hr)+'</td></tr>';
    yp+=hp;ye+=he;yt+=ht;ya+=ha;yr+=hr}
  h+='<tr class="sub"><td>년도 합계</td><td class="r">'+fmt(yp)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(ye)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(yt)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(ya)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(yr)+'</td></tr></tbody>';
  const act=document.activeElement&&document.activeElement.dataset&&document.activeElement.dataset.x;
  $('#detT').innerHTML=h;
  if(act!==undefined&&act!==''){const el=$('#detT [data-x="'+act+'"]');if(el){el.focus();try{el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length,el.value.length)}catch(e){}}}
  $$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed);
}
function redraw(){$('#prev').value=PREV?fmt(PREV):'';draw()}
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed)return;
  if(el.id==='prev'){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');PREV=parse(el.value);draw();return}
  if(el.dataset&&el.dataset.x!==undefined){EXT[+el.dataset.x]=parse(el.value);draw()}});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{if(e.target.id==='prev'&&!closed)e.target.value=PREV?fmt(PREV):''});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.id==='prev'||(e.target.dataset&&e.target.dataset.x!==undefined))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
$('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed)return;PREV=PREV_PL;redraw();toast('직전연도 부가가치세 신고서의 매출 공급가액 합계를 불러왔습니다')});
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const d=e.data;if(d&&d.type==='vat-set-closed'){closed=!!d.closed;syncLock();redraw()}});
bindCommon(redraw,null);

</script>

<script>

const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
const fmt=n=>(n<0?'-':'')+Math.abs(Math.trunc(n)).toLocaleString('en-US');
const parse=s=>{const d=String(s).replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');return d?parseInt(d,10):0};
const esc=s=>String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
let closed=false;
function toast(m,ms){const t=$('#toast');t.textContent=m;t.hidden=false;clearTimeout(toast.h);toast.h=setTimeout(()=>{t.hidden=true},ms||2200)}
function applyPeriod(){const o=$('#periodSel').selectedOptions[0];$('#dFrom').value=o.dataset.from;$('#dTo').value=o.dataset.to;$('#dueDate').textContent=o.dataset.due;}
function syncLock(){['saveBtn','delBtn','ldBtn'].forEach(i=>$('#'+i).disabled=closed);$('#modeChip').textContent=closed?'마감':'작성 중';$('#statTxt').textContent=closed?'마감':'미마감';$$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed)}
function bindCommon(redraw,validate){
  $('#periodSel').addEventListener('change',()=>{applyPeriod();redraw()});
  $('#qBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{redraw();toast('조회했습니다')});
  $('#saveBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>toast('저장되었습니다'));
  $('#printBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{try{window.print()}catch(e){}});
  const cb=$('#closeBtn');if(cb)cb.addEventListener('click',()=>{
    if(closed){closed=false;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','false');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감을 취소했습니다');return}
    const m=validate&&validate();if(m){toast(m);return}
    closed=true;$('#closeBtn').setAttribute('aria-pressed','true');$('#closeTxt').textContent='마감취소';syncLock();redraw();toast('마감되었습니다');
  });
  const ht=$('#htModal');if(ht&&$('#htBtn')){$('#htBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=false;$('#htClose').focus()});
  $('#htClose').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true});ht.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===ht)ht.hidden=true});
  $('#htGo').addEventListener('click',()=>{ht.hidden=true;toast('선택한 자료를 조회합니다')})}
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Escape')$$('.scrim').forEach(m=>m.hidden=true)});
  document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches('input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
  let th='dark';try{th=localStorage.getItem('selfbooks-theme')||'dark'}catch(e){}
  document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);
  $('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{th=th==='dark'?'light':'dark';document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme',th);try{localStorage.setItem('selfbooks-theme',th)}catch(e){}});
  applyPeriod();syncLock();redraw();
}

const BIZ=[['01','건축사'],['02','공인회계사 세무사'],['04','변리사'],['05','변호사'],['07','법무사'],['08','경영·기술지도사'],['09','감정평가사'],['11','기술사'],['13','측량사'],['14','예식장업'],['16','부동산중개업'],['17','공인노무사'],['18','의료업'],['19','수의사업'],['99','그외']];
let ROWS=[
 {id:'830512-1******',nm:'김민수',d:'2026-01-14',t:550000},
 {id:'611-45-23918',nm:'(주)한빛부동산',d:'2026-02-03',t:1650000},
 {id:'900101-2******',nm:'이서연',d:'2026-03-22',t:330000},
 {id:'',nm:'',d:'2026-04-08',t:220000}];
const TAXR=[
 {id:'107-81-62334',nm:'맛고을식당',d:'2026-01-20',s:3000000},
 {id:'305-81-17764',nm:'스마일치과',d:'2026-03-15',s:1800000}];
const ORG={cash:ROWS.length,tax:TAXR.length};
let SEL=0,TAB=1;
const vat=t=>{const s=Math.round(t/1.1);return [s,t-s]};
function drawSum(){
  const cr=ROWS.filter(r=>r.t>0),tx=TAXR;
  const cs=cr.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.t,0),ts=tx.reduce((a,r)=>a+r.s,0);
  const row=(lab,c1,a1,c2,a2)=>'<tr><td class="rl">'+lab+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c1+c2)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(a1+a2)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c1)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(a1)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(c2)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(a2)+'</td></tr>';
  $('#sumT').innerHTML='<thead><tr><th rowspan="2" style="width:90px">공급가액</th><th colspan="2">④ 합계</th><th colspan="2">⑤ 현금매출</th><th colspan="2">⑥ 세금계산서</th></tr><tr><th>건수</th><th>합계</th><th>건수</th><th>합계</th><th>건수</th><th>합계</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
   +row('입력',cr.length,cs?cr.reduce((a,r)=>a+vat(r.t)[0],0):0,tx.length,ts)
   +row('전표',ORG.cash,ORG.cash?cr.reduce((a,r)=>a+vat(r.t)[0],0):0,ORG.tax,ts)+'</tbody>';
}
function drawCash(){
  let h='<thead><tr><th rowspan="2" style="width:40px"></th><th colspan="2">⑧ 의뢰인</th><th rowspan="2" style="width:130px">⑨ 거래일자</th><th colspan="3">⑩ 거래금액</th></tr><tr><th>주민등록번호<br>(또는 사업자등록번호)</th><th>성명<br>(또는 상호)</th><th>공급대가</th><th>공급가액</th><th>부가가치세</th></tr></thead><tbody>';
  let T=0,S=0,V=0;
  ROWS.forEach((r,i)=>{const [s,v]=vat(r.t);T+=r.t;S+=s;V+=v;
   h+='<tr data-i="'+i+'"'+(i===SEL?' class="sel"':'')+'><td class="n"><input type="checkbox" class="ckb" data-ck="'+i+'" aria-label="행 선택"></td>'
    +'<td><input class="txt" data-edit data-f="id" value="'+esc(r.id)+'" aria-label="주민등록번호 또는 사업자등록번호"></td>'
    +'<td><input class="txt" data-edit data-f="nm" value="'+esc(r.nm)+'" aria-label="성명 또는 상호"></td>'
    +'<td><input class="txt" type="date" data-edit data-f="d" value="'+r.d+'" aria-label="거래일자"></td>'
    +'<td><input class="cell num" data-edit data-f="t" value="'+fmt(r.t)+'" inputmode="numeric" aria-label="공급대가"></td>'
    +'<td class="r">'+fmt(s)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v)+'</td></tr>'});
  h+='</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="4">합계 ('+ROWS.length+'건)</td><td class="r">'+fmt(T)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(S)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(V)+'</td></tr></tfoot>';
  const act=document.activeElement;const key=act&&act.dataset&&act.dataset.f?[act.closest('tr').dataset.i,act.dataset.f]:null;
  $('#cashT').innerHTML=h;
  if(key){const el=$('#cashT tr[data-i="'+key[0]+'"] [data-f="'+key[1]+'"]');if(el){el.focus();try{el.setSelectionRange(el.value.length,el.value.length)}catch(e){}}}
  $$('[data-edit]').forEach(x=>x.disabled=closed);
  if(closed)$$('#cashT .ckb').forEach(x=>x.disabled=true);
}
function drawTax(){
  let S=0,V=0;
  let h='<thead><tr><th colspan="2">⑧ 의뢰인(거래처)</th><th rowspan="2" style="width:120px">⑨ 작성일자</th><th colspan="3">⑩ 거래금액</th></tr><tr><th>사업자등록번호</th><th>상호</th><th>공급대가</th><th>공급가액</th><th>부가가치세</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
  +TAXR.map(r=>{const v=Math.floor(r.s*0.1);S+=r.s;V+=v;return '<tr><td>'+r.id+'</td><td>'+r.nm+'</td><td>'+r.d+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.s+v)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(r.s)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(v)+'</td></tr>'}).join('')
  +'</tbody><tfoot><tr><td colspan="3">합계 ('+TAXR.length+'건)</td><td class="r">'+fmt(S+V)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(S)+'</td><td class="r">'+fmt(V)+'</td></tr></tfoot>';
  $('#taxT').innerHTML=h;
}
function redraw(){drawSum();drawCash();drawTax();$('#p1').hidden=TAB!==1;$('#p2').hidden=TAB!==2;$$('.tabs3 .btn').forEach(b=>b.setAttribute('aria-pressed',String(+b.dataset.tab===TAB)))}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const tb=e.target.closest('.tabs3 .btn');if(tb){TAB=+tb.dataset.tab;redraw();return}
  const tr=e.target.closest('#cashT tbody tr');if(tr&&!e.target.closest('.ckb')){SEL=+tr.dataset.i;$$('#cashT tbody tr').forEach(r=>r.classList.toggle('sel',+r.dataset.i===SEL))}});
document.addEventListener('input',e=>{const el=e.target;if(closed||!el.dataset||!el.dataset.f)return;
  const i=+el.closest('tr').dataset.i,r=ROWS[i];
  if(el.dataset.f==='t'){el.value=el.value.replace(/[^0-9,]/g,'');r.t=parse(el.value);drawSum();
    const tr=el.closest('tr'),[s,v]=vat(r.t);tr.children[5].textContent=fmt(s);tr.children[6].textContent=fmt(v);
    const T=ROWS.reduce((a,x)=>a+x.t,0),S=ROWS.reduce((a,x)=>a+vat(x.t)[0],0);const f=$('#cashT tfoot').children[0].children;f[1].textContent=fmt(T);f[2].textContent=fmt(S);f[3].textContent=fmt(T-S);return}
  r[el.dataset.f]=el.value});
document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{const el=e.target;if(el.dataset&&el.dataset.f==='t'&&!closed)el.value=fmt(ROWS[+el.closest('tr').dataset.i].t)});
document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{if(e.target.matches&&e.target.matches('#cashT input.cell'))setTimeout(()=>e.target.select(),0)});
$('#addRow').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed)return;ROWS.push({id:'',nm:'',d:P1D(),t:0});SEL=ROWS.length-1;redraw()});
const P1D=()=>($('#dTo').value||'2026.06').replace('.','-')+'-28';
$('#delBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed)return;const ids=$$('#cashT .ckb:checked').map(c=>+c.dataset.ck);
  if(!ids.length){if(ROWS[SEL]){ROWS.splice(SEL,1);SEL=Math.max(0,Math.min(SEL,ROWS.length-1));redraw();toast('선택한 행을 삭제했습니다')}return}
  ROWS=ROWS.filter((r,i)=>!ids.includes(i));SEL=0;redraw();toast(ids.length+'건을 삭제했습니다')});
$('#biz').innerHTML=BIZ.map(b=>'<option value="'+b[0]+'"'+(b[0]==='16'?' selected':'')+'>'+b[0]+'. '+b[1]+'</option>').join('');
$('#ldBtn').addEventListener('click',()=>{if(closed)return;
  const add=[{id:'750303-1******',nm:'박지훈',d:'2026-05-12',t:880000},{id:'',nm:'',d:'2026-06-09',t:440000}];
  ROWS=ROWS.concat(add);ORG.cash=ROWS.length;redraw();toast('매입매출전표에서 현금매출(현금영수증 미발행 포함) 자료를 불러왔습니다')});
window.addEventListener('message',e=>{const d=e.data;if(d&&d.type==='vat-set-closed'){closed=!!d.closed;syncLock();redraw()}});
bindCommon(redraw,null);

</script>
